# How to read this document

This is a transition document, not a design document. It answers four questions for whoever inherits these systems: what exists in production today, what identity each piece runs under and therefore what stops working when the departing administrator's personal accounts are closed and the shared accounts change hands, what is known to be wrong or unfinished, and where the detailed design and gotcha knowledge lives.

Every statement in Parts 1–13 was taken from a live read-only scan of the systems on 4 September 2026 (BigQuery: 15 September 2026) unless it is explicitly marked otherwise. The full raw scan outputs are reproduced verbatim as Appendices A–H so that nothing depends on this summary being complete.

The detailed engineering knowledge (why flows are built the way they are, the 30+ Salesforce gotchas, the n8n build rules) is deliberately **not** re-written here. It lives in two files that are the real operating manuals, and the successor should read them before touching anything:

| File | Where | Read first |
|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE.md` (Salesforce Workspace, 224 KB, updated 2026-08-27) | `C:\Users\mrodriguez\Downloads\Salesforce Workspace\CLAUDE.md`; also Claude Project doc `01_SALESFORCE_ORG_CLAUDE.md` (2 days behind) | "Lessons learned / gotchas" (30 items), "Stage protection", gotcha #30 (2-day prod outage) |
| `CLAUDE.md` (Claude Make and N8N, 129 KB, updated 2026-08-27) | `C:\Users\mrodriguez\Downloads\Claude Make and N8N\CLAUDE.md`; also Claude Project doc `02_N8N_ENGINE_CLAUDE.md` | §0 environment facts, §0.1 (post-cutover state), §2 build gotchas |
| `04_START_HERE_INDEX.md` | Claude Project "Salesforce" | Explains which of the six FUR documents owns which kind of truth |

# Part 1 — Executive summary

**[Certain] Production today is smaller than the documentation suggests.** Of the six systems, the things that actually run unattended in production and touch business data are:

1. Salesforce prod (`00DKb000000OvoRMAS`, "HomeSi") — 209 active flows, 99 custom Apex classes, 15 active custom triggers, 30 scheduled jobs, the Encompass REST endpoint `OpportunityUpdater`, and the 360 SMS / Zoom / RingCentral / Customer.io managed packages.
2. Two n8n daily digests (LOA Work Items, Document Follow Up) that email agents every weekday at 09:00 ET, plus the WF-4 reply handler and Escalate & Notify sub-workflow, which are published but only run when called — and their caller (the Inbound Dispatcher) is **unpublished**, so no borrower email is processed today.
3. One Make.com integration (Salesforce `AI_Trigger__c` → webhook → Claude → ~28 `AI_*` fields written back to the Lead) — healthy, ~11 ops per lead.
4. Customer.io — email campaigns in workspaces 164380 and 224311, fed by a 30-minute Salesforce pull sync (Lead sync 503 / Opp sync 623) and writing engagement Tasks + opt-outs back to Salesforce.
5. AWS — the `salesforce-lambda-bridge-prod` LeadView portal backend, the `supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod` S3→Salesforce sync, the data-quality API, 3 public RDS instances, 2.44 TB of loan eFolders in `loanrepository`, ~$600/month.
6. BigQuery — project `mcp-connector-procedure`: a daily Data Transfer Service mirror of 10 Salesforce objects plus 79 views across 8 datasets that feed the SimoOS metrics app and a Lovable lead-dedupe app; owned by Google accounts on the `simologic.com` domain, not by any Simo/Supreme identity.

**[Certain] The Borrower Follow-Up (FUR) system — the largest project of 2026 — is deployed to prod but not live.** Salesforce metadata is in prod (objects, 43 fields, 4 active flows, layouts). All seven n8n workflows are bound to the prod org. But WF-1 (intake), WF-2 (outbound cadence) and WF-5 (housekeeping) have never been published, the Dispatcher is off, `fur_settings.test_mode = true`, and five gating blockers remain (compliance footer/NMLS number, unsubscribe mechanism, integration user, mailbox decisions, pilot scope). No borrower has ever received an automated email from it in production. Part 6 is the complete state.

**[Certain] The credential problem is real and concentrated.** Every n8n Salesforce node, the Claude connectors and (most likely) the Customer.io ↔ Salesforce grant authenticate through OAuth tokens minted by an interactive login on the Salesforce role account **It Support** (`005Kb00000B1HzJIAV`). That account stays active and only its email changes, so nothing breaks on day one — but a password reset with "revoke sessions", or a well-meaning cleanup that deactivates it, would take down the n8n digests, the FUR engine and the Customer.io syncs at once. Every n8n and Make error alert goes only to `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`, which will stop being read. The AWS MCP runs as root. Part 3 is the register of what to re-home or protect, in priority order.

**[Certain] Three things the successor cannot do without someone else acting first:** all 78 Salesforce licenses are consumed (It Support keeps its seat as the role account, so a personal admin login for the successor needs a seat freed or bought); the AWS organization management account `202294324213` uses a personal Gmail as master email; the Make.com account has 2FA off and no second admin.

**[Likely] The highest-probability real outage after the hand-off** is not Salesforce — it is the AWS Secrets Manager entries for `salesforce-lambda-bridge-prod` and `supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod`. If they hold a personal Salesforce or Encompass login rather than an integration identity, the Encompass→Salesforce sync and the LeadView portal API stop the day that login is closed. Nobody has verified their contents; do so first.

# Part 4 — First 30 days: what to do, in order

**Week 1 — protect what runs under shared identities.** Put a written hold on the It Support Salesforce account: do not deactivate it and do not revoke its sessions (rows 3, 6, 10). Change its email and the `Test_Recipient__c` setting (row 9). Read the three Secrets Manager entries in register row 1 and re-issue anything personal. Confirm which Salesforce user the Customer.io grant uses and re-authorise as `sf integrations` if it is anything else (row 2). Re-consent the `itsupportaccount` Outlook token and change every alert recipient in n8n and Make (rows 4, 12). Confirm the BigQuery transfer owner and its Salesforce credential (row 19). Enable 2FA on Make and add a second admin (row 12). The n8n integration-user rebind (row 3) can wait, but when you do it verify by re-reading each node's `activeVersion` JSON, not the draft — the write tool reports success even when a change lands in a dead nested copy (n8n CLAUDE.md, "setNodeParameter nests").

**Week 2 — security debt that predates you but is now yours.** Rotate the AWS root key and move root recovery to a company mailbox (row 14). Identify the `serverless` key's caller via CloudTrail and plan its retirement (row 16). Put Public Access Block on `integrator-upload-documents` (30 GB of loan documents, no PAB) and at account level. Note the three RDS instances are publicly accessible. Remove or review the vendor account **La Haus AI** (`docsadmin@lahaus.com`) which holds full System Administrator in Salesforce prod with no role, and the `Temp_Contact_Modify_All` permission set ("TEMPORARY diagnostic — remove after testing"). Purge `fur_question_log` (n8n Data Table `Qr4guHMOe50UvXul`) — it contains staff email bodies, vendor OTP codes and a card receipt swept from the shared mailbox.

**Week 3 — decide the FUR system's fate.** It is either finished (Part 6 §6.4 lists exactly what remains: compliance footer with the real NMLS number, an unsubscribe mechanism, integration user, mailbox confirmation, pilot scope) or it is switched off cleanly (deactivate the 4 prod flows via `FlowDefinition activeVersionNumber=0`, leave the objects). Do not leave it in the current half-state: WF-4 and the Escalate sub-workflow are published against prod with personal credentials, and the Dispatcher can be turned on by anyone with n8n access.

**Week 4 — cost and hygiene.** Upgrade the two RDS MySQL 8.0 instances to 8.4 to remove the $297.60/month Extended Support charge (half the AWS bill). Create an AWS Budget (none exists). Delete the 6 EventBridge rules firing at deleted Lambdas, the `alb-test`/`waf-test`, and set CloudWatch log retention (152 GB, 483 groups with none). Decide whether the 23 GoToWebinar registrations queued in Make hook 2371114 since 2026-07-17 must be back-filled to Salesforce. Put the Salesforce Workspace source tree under git — none of the six working folders is a repository today; disk is the only copy.

# Part 5 — Salesforce production

Scan date 2026-09-04; full detail in Appendix A.

## 5.1 Org facts

HomeSi, Enterprise Edition, org `00DKb000000OvoRMAS`, instance USA408, My Domain `ruby-ruby-7485`. Sandbox `homesi-staging` (`00DEm000008XXK7MAO`) is refreshed from prod and carries **real borrower emails and phones** — every send path built against it needs a test-mode gate. Staging has drifted from prod in both directions (fields that exist in one but not the other are listed throughout the Salesforce `CLAUDE.md`); never assume metadata parity.

Two apps in use: HOMESÍ-B2B (`standard__LightningSales`) and HOMESÍ-B2C (`HOMESB2C`). Both reference the prod-only flexipage `Scheduled_SMS_Record_Page1`, so neither can be deployed to staging.

## 5.2 Automation footprint

| Type | Count | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Active flows | **209** | 69 Opportunity, 34 Lead, 12 Task, 3 Work Item, 4 Opportunity Team Member, 3 Feed Item, 2 SMS History, 2 OCR, 1 each Contact/Event/FUR; 77 screen or autolaunched |
| Inactive flow definitions | 107 | ~85 legacy; two dormant Strategy duplicates are delete candidates |
| Custom Apex classes | 99 | 46 are source-controlled in the local `force-app` tree — **retrieve from prod before editing anything** (the tree is not complete) |
| Custom Apex triggers | 16 (15 active) | `LeadConversionTrigger` inactive |
| Managed triggers | 32 | 20 `tdc_tsw` (360 SMS), 12 `ZVC` (Zoom) |
| Validation rules | Opp 9 active / 11, Lead 11 / 14 | most honour `Bypass_Validation_Rules__c` and exclude `sfintegrations@citylendinginc.com` |
| Scheduled jobs | 30 | 4 scheduled flows, 1 scheduled Apex (`Update Delivery Status`, nightly, sf integrations), 14 report subscriptions (12 owned by Elias Yaber, last login 2026-01-01) |
| Workflow rules | 0 | 2 dormant workflow alerts remain |

**Opportunity carries 69 active flows plus 6 custom triggers.** Any change to Opportunity automation must assume interaction effects. The five execution-order hazards (two before-save flows have no guaranteed order; after-save recursion does not re-run before-save flows; `doesRequireRecordChangedToMeetCriteria` is not "this field changed"; a failing email action in an after-save flow rolls back the save; cross-object references in entry criteria deploy clean and throw at runtime) are written up in `01_Automation_Inventory.md` §6 and caused, respectively, wrong Strategy values, a denied loan sitting open for 5 days, 43 false milestones, a staging save outage, and the **2-day prod outage of 2026-07-28/29** (Salesforce case ErrorId 530106887). Emergency mitigation for any misbehaving flow: deploy a `FlowDefinition` with `<activeVersionNumber>0</activeVersionNumber>` — no tests, instant, reversible.

The functional clusters, each documented in depth in the Salesforce `CLAUDE.md`:

- **Stage / Current Status engine** (10 flows + 2 VRs, `Update_current_Milestone_Loan_status_Opp` v26 is the heart). Closed Won is automation-only; a funded loan cannot go Closed Lost; closed opps are never re-derived.
- **LOA2 milestone Work Items + 10 notification emails** (22 flows): Encompass writes a milestone date → email to LO + LOA2 + Loan Processor from `sfintegrations@supremelending.com` → Work Item (`Mortgage_Condition__c`, RT LOA2_Milestone_Task) if an LOA2 is assigned. Test switch = `Notification_Settings__c`. `LOA2_FHA_Docs_Email_to_Nila` is **not** under Test Mode. Notifications #4 (Appraisal POD) and #5 (Rate Lock) were never converted.
- **Assignment & sharing**: `LOA_Assignment_First_Touch` (v6) resolves LOA1/LOA2/Processor Jr from LOS text or UI lookups and creates the First Touch work item once per loan; Apex `OpportunityCoOwnerSharing` / `LeadCoOwnerSharing` rebuild Manual shares (the Lead version has a known bug that drops the other share when one field changes; the Opp version rebuilds from all four fields and now watches OwnerId).
- **Work Items (`Mortgage_Condition__c`, label "Work Item")**: OWD Private; `MortgageChecklistController` is `without sharing` on purpose; LWCs `mortgageChecklist`, `myWorkItems`, `conditionsGrid`, `addWorkItemsModal`; screen flow `Add_Work_Items` v8; Lead work items open to all 13 human profiles. A partial object-header deploy once disabled reports on the object — always include every `enable*` flag.
- **SLA system**: `SLA_Follow_Ups` v9 scores Tasks by `Touch_Type__c`; `SLA_Aging_9AM/5PM`, `SLA_Task_Aging` scheduled flows. LOA first-touch moved to work items, so SLA coverage on LOA first touch is gone until an SLA-v2 on work items is built.
- **NPPM referral chain**: `Set_NPPM_From_Referral_Chain` (Opp v3) and `_Lead` (v1) walk Contact → Realtor Opp → `NPPM__c` by Ids only; guardrails leave fields untouched on unstructured data. Backfill of ~101 blank loans was never run.
- **Encompass integration**: `OpportunityUpdater` `@RestResource(/OpportunityUpdate/*)` — match precedence LoanId → Loan# → Branch+email/phone on `Phone_360SMS__c`; junk-placeholder filter; Branch Transfer guard; 12 tests. The caller is the AWS `supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod` stack (Part 10).
- **Email capture**: `EmailMessageStubContactCreator` creates stub Contacts on outbound so third-party replies are captured; capture is gated on address match.
- **Data quality**: `LeadDataQualityTrigger` enqueues one Queueable per lead — >50 per transaction is fatal on bulk loads; holders of perm set `Bypass_Lead_Automation` skip it.
- **Prequalification screen flow** `Prequalification_Form` (bilingual, Lead quick action) — staging only as of the docs; confirm prod state before promising it.
- **Batch Assign Lead Role** screen flow behind a URL list button; access via perm set `Sales_Agent_Lead` / custom permission `Batch_Assign_Lead_Role`; runs `SystemModeWithoutSharing`.

## 5.7 The It Support account after the hand-off

It Support (`005Kb00000B1HzJIAV`) is a role account, not a personal user, and it remains active. The only change is its email address, currently `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`. Two rules protect everything that hangs off it: (1) change the email and password normally but **never tick "revoke sessions" and never deactivate** — the n8n Salesforce credentials, the Claude connectors, the 360 SMS / Kixie / RingCentral connected-app tokens and very likely the Customer.io grant are OAuth tokens minted by interactive logins on this account, and revocation kills them all at once; (2) if the account is ever retired in future, do it in this order: reassign the 35 dashboard running users, move the private reports and owned Work Items, re-authorise every external OAuth as `sf integrations` or a dedicated Integration-license user, then freeze for 30 days before deactivating. The `sf integrations` user (`005Qg00000C9srvIAB`) already exists and is the right home for anything unattended; a shared human login is a weak audit trail for automation.

# Part 6 — Borrower Follow-Up (FUR) system — exact state

This is the 2026 flagship project: an n8n engine that chases borrowers for documents by email, classifies their replies with Claude, files attachments to Salesforce, and escalates questions to agents as Work Items. Design: `03_DESIGN_SPEC.md` (DECIDED markers are final). Truth about what is proven: `07_TEST_EVIDENCE.md`. What is wrong: `06_OPEN_DEFECTS.md`. What must happen to go live: `08_REMAINING_CUTOVER.md`. Production log: `05_PROD_STATE_LOG.md`. **The header of `08` and §0 of the design spec still say "nothing in prod" — they are stale.**

## 6.4 What stands between "deployed" and "live"

Gating blockers (`08_REMAINING_CUTOVER` §0): real email templates with the compliance footer — today WF-2 prints `NMLS #—` literally and promises an unsubscribe link that does not exist (0.1); integration user replacing the personal Salesforce login (0.2); mailbox chain confirmed end-to-end (0.3); pilot scope — one branch, 2–3 agents, agent-initiated only (0.4); decision on whether the AI Chatter post on an Opp should keep triggering `Chatter to Contacted Flow` (0.5).

Then, in this order: assign PermSet `0PSQg000000JTUXOA4` + Work-Item Task RT to the integration user's profile; FLS on 13 profiles; repoint WF-2 send nodes to `docs@`; set WF-5 digest recipient; publish Escalate sub → WF-1 A/B → WF-2 → WF-5, then the Dispatcher; run the 15 smoke tests in `08` §3 (none ticked — 3.9 "two replies same minute → two executions" is the most important); only then `fur_settings.test_mode=false`. Rollback: `test_mode=true` is the kill switch; full stop = unpublish WF-1/2/4/5; Salesforce emergency = FlowDefinition `activeVersionNumber=0`.

Known gaps launching with (accepted): no frequency cap or 4-hour dedupe, quiet hours in one timezone (ET), no state→TZ map, no SMS (Phase 2, 360 SMS key not in n8n), no SMS opt-out propagation (`tdc_tsw__SMS_Opt_out__c` not API-visible to the integration user), no document-level verification (WF-4 v3), no FAQ auto-reply (deliberate), nothing closes a FUR when its Opportunity closes (WF-5's 14-day stale rule catches it), Message-ID match rung not built, WF-4 `Get FUR` lacks `alwaysOutputData`. The cadence engine has **never run unattended on its own schedule**; a one-week soak with `test_mode=true` was proposed and not done.

Two locked scratch workflows (`My workflow 6`, `My workflow 7`) need checking in the UI.

**Live production automations (their own trigger fires):**

| Workflow | Id | Schedule | What it does | Credentials |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| LOA Work Items - Daily Digest (per owner) | `sEZI3Hg6CEYLFokc` | weekdays 09:00 ET | SOQL open `Mortgage_Condition__c` due ≤ today, excluding owners It Support and sf integrations → one Outlook email per owner, cc team leads | Salesforce `quBZzjgLYxZSi9My` (personal), Outlook `9SSXP90Befj1zdrm` |
| Document Follow Up - Daily Digest (per owner) | `0tIUhttqlyLIB4Is` | weekdays 09:00 ET | Open Tasks `Subject LIKE 'Document Follow Up%'` due ≤ today → one email per owner | Salesforce `dsfRbykdxZPdoIte` (personal, second cred of the same name), Outlook `9SSXP90Befj1zdrm` |
| Error Alert (shared) | `5gSRY0P5hpfLKmFD` | on error | Emails `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`; the 2026-08-28 classifier draft was never published | Outlook `9SSXP90Befj1zdrm` |

Both digests: 15/15 recent successes. Canonical shape (Schedule → Salesforce query → Code → Outlook) is the template for any new per-owner digest.

Credentials (12) are tabulated in Appendix B §3. The five that matter are all interactive OAuth tokens minted by the departing admin. Gateway credits are available on the instance for Anthropic/OpenAI nodes and could replace the personal Anthropic key.

Build rules that cost days to learn and are non-negotiable on this instance (full list in n8n `CLAUDE.md` §2): never use `setNodeParameter` — it nests a dead `parameters.parameters` copy and the node keeps running old code; use `updateNodeParameters` with `replace:true` and re-read `activeVersion` after every write; `create_workflow_from_code` auto-binds the first Salesforce credential regardless of what you ask for; a pinned `test_workflow` can never validate a SOQL SELECT list, a credential, or a send; never clear a Salesforce field with a literal `""` (bind an expression that resolves empty); Salesforce query nodes fed multiple items flatten their results, so zero-row items vanish silently — index by key in a Code node, never branch an If directly off a per-item query; every workflow must have Error Workflow = `5gSRY0P5hpfLKmFD`.

A scoped IAM plan for the FHA-binder MCP user already exists (`Claude AWS/AWS-MCP-Setup-FHA-Binder-Submitter.md` + policy JSON); it was never created.

# Part 11 — BigQuery

Live scan 2026-09-15 (Appendix F). Project **`mcp-connector-procedure`** (number 804513710204), all datasets in US, ~1.57 GB, no reservations, no scheduled queries, no materialized views.

| Dataset | Contents | Owner (dataset ACL) |
|---|---|---|
| `salesforce` | 10 tables mirroring Salesforce prod: Account, Contact, Lead (183 k), Loan_Officer__c, NPPM__c, Opportunity, Profile, RecordType, Task (2.72 M rows / 1.26 GB), User — daily full refresh, no soft-deleted rows | `itsupportaccount@simologic.com` |
| `home_si` | 1 table + 6 views — curated Salesforce serving layer | `itsupportaccount@simologic.com` |
| `app_b2b_metrics` | 5 views + 2 routines (`norm` UDF, `realtor_metrics` table function) — Metrics B2B app v1 | `icano@simologic.com` |
| `b2b_marts` | 7 views — Metrics B2B app v2 | `icano@simologic.com` |
| `lending_marts` | 8 tables + 35 views — company-wide lending star schema; includes `pipeline_snapshot_stage` (Salesforce Forecast Pipeline report, uploaded 2–3×/day) and `encompass_loans_stage` (Encompass loan export, 5,502 rows) | `icano@simologic.com` |
| `comp_marts` | 6 tables + 9 views — Compensafe payroll / commissions | `icano@simologic.com` |
| `hr_centralizado` | 14 tables + 16 views — HR rosters; `active_roster_raw` (270 rows) still holds national IDs, bank accounts, home addresses, birth dates — restrict or delete | `icano@simologic.com` |
| `governance` | 4 tables — frozen baselines, 60-day partition expiry | `icano@simologic.com` |

**How data gets in.** The Salesforce mirror is loaded by the BigQuery **Data Transfer Service** (data source `salesforce`, config `6a6b8803-0000-2016-a2f4-582429a8c29c`), running as the Google-managed agent `service-804513710204@gcp-sa-bigquerydatatransfer.iam.gserviceaccount.com`, daily at ~05:02 UTC — 894 loads, 0 errors since 2026-06-13. The transfer config itself was created under a human Google account (the dataset owner `itsupportaccount@simologic.com`) and carries its own Salesforce OAuth credential; which Salesforce user that is can only be seen in the console (BigQuery → Data transfers → config → Update credentials). **The transfer did not run on 12 and 13 September**; a manual run on the 14th caught up. Failed DTS runs leave no BigQuery job, so credential problems surface only in the transfer's Run history — check it weekly. Everything else is pushed by the SimoOS uploader through service account `simo-sync-bq-writer@mcp-connector-procedure.iam.gserviceaccount.com` (72 loads in 30 days) or created interactively by the data architect `icano@simologic.com` (all DDL — 159 CREATE VIEW, CTAS, override-table edits — via the MCP server).

**Who reads it.** `lovable-bq-reader@…` (1,471 SELECTs / 30 days, only `salesforce.Lead/Contact/Account` — the Lovable lead-dedupe app), `simo-sync-bq-reader@…` (1,191 — the SimoOS backend, reads every mart right after each upload), `icano@` ad hoc, `simoos-app-bigquery@…` (dormant since 2026-08-07), and the Claude BigQuery connector as `itsupportaccount@simologic.com`. The HOMESÍ Analytics Agent (`14_BIGQUERY_AGENT_PROMPT.md`) sits on `salesforce` and `home_si`. No Customer.io data exists anywhere in the project.

**Freshness.** `salesforce.*` last loaded 2026-09-15 05:02 UTC; `MAX(SystemModstamp)` Task 04:26, Lead 01:29, Account/Contact 00:49, Opportunity 00:12 — normally ≤ 24 h + ~4 h lag; on 13–14 September the mirror was up to ~58 h stale. Payroll and HR tables are period uploads (last 2026-08-27 … 09-03).

**Identity facts that matter.** No `@simosolutionsgroup.com`, `@citylendinginc.com` or `@supremelending.com` account holds anything in this project — every human right belongs to the `simologic.com` domain. Removing `itsupportaccount@simologic.com` orphans the DTS config, its Salesforce credential, the Claude connector and the Analytics Agent; once the transfer fails, `salesforce.*` freezes and all `home_si`, `app_b2b_metrics`, `b2b_marts` and most `lending_marts` views go stale while Lovable and SimoOS keep serving old data (SimoOS probes `salesforce.__TABLES__` and will show a freshness warning; Lovable will not). Removing `icano@simologic.com` stops nothing scheduled but leaves six datasets with no human owner (recoverable via projectOwners). Service-account keys for `simo-sync-bq-writer/reader`, `lovable-bq-reader`, `b2b-metrics-sync` and `simoos-app-bigquery` live inside the apps (Lovable secrets, Vercel WIF in `BigQ/simo-os.env`, the SimoOS uploader) and are the next thing to inventory; the last two look retired. Project-level IAM and the billing owner still need a console check. The full SQL of all 79 views is in the handover pack (`01_scan_reports/bigquery.md`, Appendix A) as insurance.

#### 2.2 Inactive flow definitions (IsActive = false) — 107

Query: `SELECT ApiName, Label, TriggerObjectOrEventLabel, ProcessType, LastModifiedDate FROM FlowDefinitionView WHERE IsActive = false ORDER BY ApiName LIMIT 200` → **107 rows**. Of these, ~22 are Salesforce-shipped templates with no LastModifiedDate (`se_*`, `sfdc_fieldservice__*`, `agentic_feed__*`, `setup_service_plan__*`, `identity_flows__*`, `opencti__*`, `sflu__*`); the remaining ~85 are org-built flows that have been deactivated (many are the pre-2026 Lead/Opportunity "contacted"/"queue"/"owner" flows superseded by newer logic). ### 14. Queries that failed (and why)

| Query | Tool | Error / resolution |
|---|---|---|
| `SELECT Name, DeveloperName, CreatedBy.Name, CreatedDate FROM ConnectedApplication` | hosted | `INVALID_FIELD: No such column 'DeveloperName'` → re-run without DeveloperName (27 rows). |
| `SELECT … FROM ApexClass` / `ApexTrigger` (Tooling) with `directory=/home/claude` | Salesforce DX | `ENOENT: chdir 'C:\Windows\System32' -> '/home/claude'` — the DX MCP runs on the user's Windows device; re-run with `directory=C:\Users\mrodriguez\Downloads\Salesforce Workspace` succeeded. |
| `SELECT Id, Name, Type, UsageType, Status, CreatedBy.Name FROM Group` | hosted | `No such column 'UsageType'` → re-run with `DeveloperName, Email` (91 rows). |
| `SELECT Id, DeveloperName, MasterLabel, Description, ManagerId FROM Territory2Model` | hosted | `INVALID_TYPE: sObject type 'Territory2Model' is not supported` — Enterprise Territory Management not enabled. |
| `SELECT Id, DeveloperName, MasterLabel, Status, Location FROM ExternalDataSource` and `…EndpointUrl…` (Tooling) | both | `No such column 'Status'` / `'EndpointUrl'` — not re-attempted (no external objects present). |
| `SELECT Id, DeveloperName, MasterLabel, IsActive FROM AuthProvider` | hosted | `No such column 'MasterLabel'`; Tooling: `sObject type 'AuthProvider' is not supported`; data API `SELECT Id, DeveloperName, FriendlyName, ProviderType FROM AuthProvider` → 0 rows (none configured). |
| `SELECT Id, DeveloperName, EntityDefinitionId, TemplateId, CcEmails, SenderType, SenderAddress FROM WorkflowAlert` (Tooling) | DX | `No such column 'SenderAddress'` → re-run without it (2 rows). |
| `SELECT Id, Name, TableEnumOrId, Active, LastModifiedDate FROM WorkflowRule` (Tooling) | DX | `No such column 'Active'` → re-run without it → 0 rows. |
| `SELECT Id, Name, ApexClass.Name, JobType, Status … FROM AsyncApexJob` | hosted | `No such column 'Name' on AsyncApexJob` → used GROUP BY summary instead. |
| `SELECT Id, Name, Type, Status, CreatedBy.Name FROM ListEmail` | hosted | `No such column 'Type'` — dropped (not needed). |
| `SELECT Id, ContentType, Name, Value, CreatedBy.Name FROM Document` | hosted | `No such column 'Value'` — dropped. |
| `SELECT Id, Name, IsPersonal, LoginIpRanges FROM Profile` | hosted | `No such column 'IsPersonal'` → `SELECT Id, Name FROM Profile WHERE Name='System Administrator'` (1 row). |
| `SELECT … FROM FlowDefinitionView WHERE IsActive = true … LIMIT 200` | hosted | Not an error, but result (72 KB) exceeded the tool's inline limit and was written to a file; second page fetched with `OFFSET 200` (9 rows). Same for Report (two pages) and Dashboard (137 rows) results. |
| `mcp__Salesforce_-_Beta__*` server | — | Failed to connect (`405 CLIENT_HTTP_NOT_IMPLEMENTED`); not needed, hosted sObject + DX tools covered everything. |

### 15. It Support account (`005Kb00000B1HzJIAV`) — what depends on it, and the rule that follows

**It Support is a role account and stays active after the hand-off; only its email (`m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`) changes.** The items below are therefore not things that break on departure — they are the reasons the account must be handled as infrastructure: **change its email and password normally, never revoke its sessions, never deactivate it without first re-homing every item here.**

1. **35 dashboards run as It Support** (SpecifiedUser) — 16 in the B2C & B2B Dashboards folder (`Closing On Time - Branch` `01ZQg00000MH3EXMA1`, `Days in Current Stage` `01ZQg00000QlXmLMAV`, Active Pipeline ×2, Closings ×3, Pre-Approval, Ratified, SLA by Stage, Follow Ups, Meeting Attendance/Scheduled, Pre-Quals, Lead Management History), all 4 LOA Dashboards, both B2B Realtor dashboards, 13 packaged/legacy. They keep working while the account is active; Salesforce refuses to run a dashboard whose running user is inactive. Regenerate the list with the Dashboard query in §10.
2. **13 reports in its private folder** are reachable only by whoever logs in as It Support; 306 reports total were created or last modified by it (public folders are unaffected).
3. **6 CronTriggers created by it** — 3 scheduled flows (`SLA_Aging_9AM/5PM`, `SLA_Task_Aging`), 2 sitemap jobs, 1 Metalytics job. Unaffected while active.
4. **Email-bound settings to update with the address change:** the User record email itself (Salesforce sends a verification link to the new address; the new owner must click it), and `Notification_Settings__c.Test_Recipient__c` (currently the same address).
5. **OAuth tokens minted by interactive logins on this account** — the n8n credentials "Salesforce - Prod" (`quBZzjgLYxZSi9My`, `dsfRbykdxZPdoIte`) and "Salesforce - Sandbox" (`8j2Z1Q8YwHHvR9nn`), the hosted Salesforce MCP connectors used by Claude, and the connected-app grants for 360 SMS Connect, Kixie, RingCentral Webinar and Salesforce Lead Capture. These survive an email or password change but **not** "revoke all sessions" or deactivation. The Customer.io Pipelines grant `salesforce_eca` is probably in the same category (confirm in the Customer.io UI). Long-term fix: re-home unattended integrations onto `sf integrations` (`005Qg00000C9srvIAB`) or a dedicated Salesforce Integration-license user (5 free seats).
6. **Records owned by It Support:** 2,800 Contacts, 35 Accounts, 26 Leads, 6 Opportunities, 21 open Work Items (Private OWD — invisible to LOAs until reassigned), 12 open Tasks. Only the Work Items need action.
7. **License:** It Support keeps one of the 78/78 Salesforce seats. A personal admin login for the successor needs a seat freed or purchased.
8. Items **not** tied to It Support but flagged for the successor: `Temp_Contact_Modify_All` permission set still exists; vendor account `La Haus AI` (docsadmin@lahaus.com) holds full System Administrator; Elias Yaber (System Administrator, last login 2026-01-01) owns 36 dashboards and 12 of the 30 scheduled jobs.

#### 2.2 Per-workflow detail

Legend for credential labels: **[PROD-SF quBZ]** = `quBZzjgLYxZSi9My` "Salesforce - Prod"; **[PROD-SF dsfR]** = `dsfRbykdxZPdoIte` "Salesforce - Prod" (second cred with the same name); **[OL-itsupport]** = `9SSXP90Befj1zdrm` Outlook `itsupportaccount@simosolutionsgroup.com`; **[OL-docs]** = `X9T7HveknhfWyJd5` Outlook "Docs - Homesi" (`docs@homesi.co`); **[Anthropic]** = `eP6vFFiBqcaHXKmU` "Anthropic account".

##### 1. `7nVnALsJHaPseTVt` — FUR WF-4 Email Reply Handler (staging) — ACTIVE (callable)
**What it does.** Processes ONE inbound borrower email per execution (handed over by the Dispatcher with inputs `id, subject, from, bodyPreview, hasAttachments, internetMessageId`). It first checks the Salesforce `Inbound_Message__c` ledger for the RFC-822 Message-ID (idempotency; sentinel `'NO-MESSAGE-ID'` when blank) and dead-ends if already processed. Otherwise it extracts the `[FUR-xxx]` thread token and runs a match waterfall (token → Opportunity email → Lead email; ambiguity = unmatched), re-reads the FUR, has Claude Sonnet 4.6 classify the reply into independent booleans (opt_out / not_interested / has_question / wants_call / will_send / sending_docs + summary + borrower_message, language preserved), computes the FUR state change in `Decide Update`, writes the FUR (`Status__c, Last_Inbound_At__c, First_Response_At__c, Next_Touch_At__c, Escalation_Reason__c, Outcome__c, AI_Summary__c`), then fans out: uploads non-inline attachments to the FUR via `document:upload` (`linkToObjectId=furId`), escalates to the sub-workflow + logs the question to the `fur_question_log` data table if Escalated, propagates opt-out to `Opportunity.HasOptedOutOfEmail__c` / `Lead.HasOptedOutOfEmail` via REST PATCH, and inserts the `Inbound_Message__c` ledger row (Matched). Unmatched mail is logged to the data table, emailed to m.rodriguez, ledgered (Unmatched), and — if a single Lead/Opp record could be identified but had >1 open FUR — the record gets `Needs_Agent__c=true`, a Chatter post, and any attachments uploaded to the record itself. A `Soft-Failure Check` scans the five best-effort (`onError: continueRegularOutput`) nodes and emails m.rodriguez if any swallowed an error.
**Nodes/credentials.**
- Trigger: `New Email (docs inbox)` = `executeWorkflowTrigger` (NOT an Outlook trigger).
- Salesforce **[PROD-SF quBZ]**: `Check Already Processed` (alwaysOutputData); `Match by Token` ; `Match by Opp Email` (executeOnce); `Match by Lead Email` (same on `Lead__r.Email`); `Get FUR` (by Id, LIMIT 1); `Update FUR` (customObject update `Follow_Up_Request__c`); `Upload File` and `Upload To Record` (document upload); `Ledger (Matched)` / `Ledger (Unmatched)` (create `Inbound_Message__c`).
- HTTP **[PROD-SF quBZ]** to `https://ruby-ruby-7485.my.salesforce.com/services/data/v59.0/…`: `Flag Needs Agent` (PATCH `/sobjects/{recordType}/{recordId}` `{"Needs_Agent__c":true}`), `Chatter Record` (POST `/chatter/feed-elements`), `Propagate Opt-Out` (PATCH `/sobjects/{recordType}/{recordId}`). All `onError: continueRegularOutput`.
- Outlook **[OL-docs]**: `Get Attachments`, `Download Attachment`, `Get Unrouted Attachments`, `Download Unrouted Attachment` (all keyed on the Dispatcher's Graph message id — must stay on the same mailbox the Dispatcher polls).
- Outlook **[OL-itsupport]**: `Notify Unmatched` → `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`; `Soft-Failure Alert` → `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`.
- AI **[Anthropic]**: `Claude` (`lmChatAnthropic`, claude-sonnet-4-6, temp 0) feeding `Classify Reply` (Information Extractor).
- Data table: `Log Question`, `Log Unmatched` → `fur_question_log` (`Qr4guHMOe50UvXul`).
- Sub-workflow: `Escalate & Notify` → `vJKvJpOZUxrhqOKY` (mode each, onError continue).
**State note.** Draft `versionId 1467139a…` differs from `activeVersionId 05771f4e…` only by n8n normalization noise (dropped default `resource/operation` on `Log Unmatched`, empty `notice`/`leftValue` keys). Node set and connections are identical. Last executions: 458/456 success (2026-08-27, via harness), 453 error (2026-08-27 — the Anthropic "credit balance too low" incident).

##### 8. `nG8hwck9RsoJZiQb` — FUR WF-2 Cadence Engine (email, staging) — inactive
The outbound borrower-email engine. `Every 15 Min` → Data Tables `Get Settings` (`fur_settings`) and `Get Cadence Steps` (`fur_cadences`) → three static condition queries **[PROD-SF quBZ]**: `Get Chase Conditions` , `Get Unchased Conditions` (`… Chase_With_Borrower__c=false …`), `Get Blank-Ask Conditions` (`… Chase_With_Borrower__c=true AND Borrower_Doc_Ask__c = null …`) → `Poll Due FURs`  → `Build Messages` Code (gates: closed Opp, Lead/Opp email opt-out, Channel_Email, quiet hours Sun/09–19 in `fallback_tz`; inline EN/ES templates for docs/interest/appt/react steps; footer literally prints `NMLS #—` and promises an unsubscribe link that is not rendered; `test_mode` recipient override to `test_email`) → `Needs Wording?` → `Escalate Missing Wording` (sub `vJKvJpOZUxrhqOKY`) / `Docs Left?` → `Complete FUR` **[PROD-SF quBZ]** (Status Completed, Outcome 'All Docs Received' or 'Chase Cancelled') or the send chain: `Create Draft` Outlook **[OL-itsupport]** (draft in the itsupportaccount mailbox, to `recipient`) → `Mark FUR (before send)` **[PROD-SF quBZ]** (Cadence_Step+1, Status Awaiting Reply, Last_Outbound_At, Next_Touch_At) → `Record Outbound Msg` **[PROD-SF quBZ]** (create `Outbound_Message__c` with `internetMessageId`) → `Send Draft` Outlook **[OL-itsupport]**. `retryOnFail` is not set on the two Outlook nodes.
**Handover flags:** (a) the send nodes are still on `itsupportaccount@simosolutionsgroup.com`, NOT the `docs@homesi.co` mailbox the Dispatcher polls — if published as-is, borrower replies would go to the wrong mailbox and never be matched; (b) `fur_settings.test_mode` is **true** with `test_email = m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`, so all sends would currently be redirected to that personal address.

### 5. Findings that matter for handover (ranked)

1. **Inbound pipeline is OFF.** The Dispatcher (`pA2IVtxuaTyFIQGK`), the only Outlook poller, is unpublished (`activeVersionId: null`, last touched 2026-09-04 15:58Z) and has not polled since 2026-08-26 22:03Z. Anything sent to `docs@homesi.co` since then is sitting unprocessed. WF-4 being "active" is meaningless without it.
2. **Outbound pipeline (WF-2) was never published**, `fur_settings.test_mode=true`, and its send nodes still use `itsupportaccount@…` rather than `docs@homesi.co`. Nothing borrower-facing is being sent. The only live production automations are the two 9 am digests and the JOGA Instagram auto-reply.
3. **Everything is bound to production Salesforce under a personal OAuth login** despite "(staging)" names. `quBZzjgLYxZSi9My` (17 nodes across 6 workflows + LOA digest) and `dsfRbykdxZPdoIte` (Document Follow Up digest) are both labelled "Salesforce - Prod" and both are Melquiades' credentials. No integration-user credential exists in n8n.
4. **Single-user project, single-inbox alerting.** All Homesi assets live in the `itsupportaccount` personal project (team projects are licensed but unused). Every alert (Error Alert, Soft-Failure, Unmatched, WF-5 digest, canary) goes only to `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com` through the `itsupportaccount` Outlook token. Deprovisioning that user breaks the workflows AND the mechanism that would report the breakage.
5. **Error Alert published version is stale** — the classifier draft from 2026-08-28 (credits/auth/rate-limit tagging) was never published; live alerts are the old generic 2-node version.
6. **Anthropic canary never activated** (`Jd5hgfTQbYexn67F`), so the 2026-08-27 credits outage would recur silently.
7. **Hardcoded personal/org identifiers in code:** default owner `005Kb00000B1HzJIAV` (Melquiades) in Escalate sub `Prep` and Path A `Plan Wording Item`; LOA digest excludes owners `005Kb00000B1HzJIAV`,`005Qg00000C9srvIAB`; prod Task RT `012Qg000003uX4PIAU` in 5 create nodes; prod pod URL in 5 HTTP nodes; digest cc lists (`pier.laino`, `alejandra.murillo`, `carlos.alvarez`) inline in Outlook nodes.

9. **`fur_question_log` contains internal-staff email bodies, vendor OTP codes and a payment receipt** swept from the shared mailbox — purge before handover.
10. **JOGA/Instagram assets are an unrelated side business** (JOGA Financial / JOGA Homes / La Gemma Reg D offering) living on the company n8n instance under Jorge Campodonico's personal project, one of which is live and depends on a community node with signature verification disabled. Decide whether they belong here at all.

#### 2.2 Scenario detail

##### `4191016` — Integration Webhooks, Salesforce (ACTIVE)
- **Modules (in order):** ① `gateway:CustomWebHook` (hook 1909871, data structure `Salesforce Data` 285827: recordId, name, record_type required; website/phone/company/state/email/nmls_id optional) → ② `gateway:WebhookRespond` (200 `{"accepted": true}`) → ③ `http:MakeRequest` POST to `https://hook.us2.make.com/3wykafmk81vg2xu5aic4iwhdhlcp3uux` (the Realtor Worker webhook), body = same 9 fields, auth = API-key keychain `SF API` (119657) sent as header.
- **What it does:** a thin ingress/relay. Salesforce (a flow/Apex callout on Lead when `AI_Trigger__c` is set) POSTs a Lead's identity fields to this webhook; it acknowledges immediately and re-posts the payload to the Realtor Worker webhook so the heavy AI work runs in a separate, rate-limited (10/min) scenario. 3 ops per run. Sample payload seen: a Loan Officer lead (`00QQg00000cC6a1MAC`, Kathryn Tan, loanDepot, NMLS 355468).
- **Hardcoded:** Make hook URL above. No SF instance URL (Salesforce initiates the call). No emails.
- **Connections:** none (webhook + HTTP with keychain). **Keys:** `SF API` 119657.

##### `4700207` — Realtor Worker (copy) (ACTIVE)
- **Modules (in order, error handlers indented):**
  1. `gateway:CustomWebHook` (hook 1941441)
  2. `util:SetVariables` — builds two large prompts (`realtor prompt`, `lo prompt`) → onerror: `datastore:AddRecord` (log FAILED) + `builtin:Ignore`
  3. `util:SetVariable2` `prompt` = realtor or LO prompt by `record_type` → onerror log + Ignore
  4. `http:MakeRequest` POST `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` (model **claude-sonnet-4-6**, header `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, auth keychain `Claude` 119644) → onerror: log → `salesforce:updateARecord` (Lead `AI_Status__c='Error'`, `AI_Trigger__c=false`, `AI_Last_Run__c=now`) → `microsoft-email:createAndSendAMessage` ("⚠️ Realtor Profile Automation - Error Alert" **to m.rodriguez@supremelending.com**) → `builtin:Break`
  5. `util:SetVariable2` `Claude Response` (extract text block) → onerror log + Ignore
  6. `util:SetVariable2` `Clean JSON` (strip non-JSON wrapper) → onerror log + Ignore
  7. `json:ParseJSON` (data structure 290972 "My data structure": profileSummary, marketFocus, serviceAreas, priceRange, specialties, interests, tone, engagementTips, linkedIn, facebook, instagram, youtube, website, zillowUrl, realtorComUrl, redfinUrl, licenseNumber, licenseState) → onerror log → `salesforce:updateARecord` (mark Lead Error) → Resume/Ignore
  8. `salesforce:updateARecord` — final Lead write: `Website`, `Tone__c`, `Notes__c`, `YouTube__c`, `AI_Status__c='Completed'`, `Bilingual__c`, `Instagram__c`, `Interests__c`, `Linked_In__c`, `Redfin_URL__c`, `Zillow_URL__c`, `AI_Last_Run__c`, `Jungo_Notes__c='SiMo Solutions'`, `Price_Range__c`, `Specialties__c`, `Market_Focus__c`, `License_State__c`, `Service_Areas__c`, `AI_NMLS_Number__c`, `License_Number__c`, `AI_employerType__c`, `Engagement_Tips__c`, `Profile_Summary__c`, `Realtor_com_URL__c`, `AI_Loan_Products__c`, `AI_Verified_Email__c`, `AI_NMLS_Profile_URL__c`, `AI_Email_Last_Seen_URL__c` → onerror log + Ignore
- **What it does:** the AI enrichment worker. For each Realtor or Loan Officer Lead relayed from `4191016`, it asks Claude (with web-research-style prompt) to build a structured public profile (social links, Zillow/Realtor.com/Redfin, license, NMLS, market focus, engagement tips, etc.), parses the JSON and writes ~28 `AI_*`/profile fields back onto the Lead in **prod** Salesforce, flipping `AI_Status__c` to Completed (or Error) and clearing `AI_Trigger__c`. Every failure path writes a row to the `HOMESI Integration Log` data store; Anthropic-call failures also email an alert. ~8 ops and 30–75 s per run (Claude latency). This is the only scenario that consumes real budget (1,729 ops in the window).
- **Hardcoded:** `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages`; error-alert recipient **m.rodriguez@supremelending.com**; example URLs baked into the prompt as few-shot samples (`https://new.mmi.run/lo/933344`, `https://www.rockmtg.com`, `https://www.linkedin.com/in/sael-torres-7135301b2/`, sample emails `jane.smith@uwm.com`, `francisco@cuevascapital.com`, `first.last@company.com`).
- **Connections:** `Salesforce Prod` 7666007, `SF SimoSolution` (Microsoft) 8052684. **Keys:** `Claude` 119644. **Data store:** 99978.
- **Health:** last 50+ executions all status 1 (success); 1 error and 3 DLQ items lifetime.

### 8. Findings / alarms for the handover

1. **Only one real integration is alive:** Salesforce → webhook `1909871` → relay → Realtor Worker → Claude → Lead update. It is healthy (100% success on the last 50+ runs) and uses the **integration user** SF connection and the **shared IT mailbox** Outlook connection, so it does **not** break when the departing user is deprovisioned — provided `itsupportaccount@simosolutionsgroup.com` (which owns the whole Make account, all connections, keys and hooks) is retained or ownership transferred.
2. **Single point of failure = the Make login itself.** Everything is authored by user 6925426 (`itsupportaccount@…`). 2FA is off on that user. There is no second admin in the org.
3. **Error alerts for the live scenario go only to `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`** (Realtor Worker module 52). After deprovisioning, Anthropic/API failures will alert nobody. Repoint before the mailbox closes.
4. **Personal credentials present:** `SF Sanbox` (7505192, Melquiadez Rodriguez staging login) and `City Webinar Creds` (9109261, `mrodriguez@citylendinginc.com` Zoom). Both back only inactive/unused scenarios — safe to delete, but they will show as broken once the accounts are gone.
5. **Plaintext secret in blueprints:** both Homesi Copilot scenarios hardcode an `X-Api-Key` for `https://ruby-ruby-7485.my.salesforce-sites.com/Webhook/services/apexrest/api/invokeWebhook` (the org's 360 SMS Apex webhook) inside the HTTP module. Anyone who can view the scenario can read it. Rotate or move to the key vault before sharing exports.
6. **Do not reactivate Homesi Copilot (4681616).** It writes `Lead.Needs_Agent__c`, which has since been repurposed by the n8n FUR escalation system; its poll watermark is hardcoded to 2026-05-14, so a reactivation would re-process ~4 months of Chatter posts, create Tasks, and send SMS/emails.
7. **Do not reactivate LOA Daily Digest (5483698).** Schedule is every 15 minutes (not daily), recipient is hardcoded to the test address, and the same digest is live in n8n (`sEZI3Hg6CEYLFokc`).
8. **Webinars Connector (5205772) is broken and leaking data:** GoToWebinar connection expired 2026-07-17, scenario is `isinvalid`, and hook `2371114` holds **23 unprocessed registrations**. Decide whether those registrants must be back-filled to Salesforce (or accept the loss) — the queue is not visible from Salesforce.
9. **Orphans safe to remove:** hooks 1973559 "Make", 2141497 "Sandbox", 2371096/2371106 (gone GoToWebinar hooks); connections 7508479 (Anthropic app), 8291967 (Make AI Provider); data structure 289165 (duplicate).
10. **Budget:** Core plan, 10,000 ops/month, 2,365 consumed this cycle — ample headroom; the live pair costs ~11 ops per lead enriched.
11. **Cross-reference — Salesforce side of the live integration:** the trigger lives in Salesforce (Lead `AI_Trigger__c` → callout to hook 1909871 with header `x-make-apikey`). Anyone taking over must locate that flow/Apex callout and the Named Credential/Remote Site holding the Make API key (`SF API 1`/`SF API 2`) — if it is rotated in Make it must be rotated there too.

#### 3.2 Key bucket details

**`loanrepository`** (created 2025-12-01) — the loan eFolder archive used by the FHA LRS binder workflow.
- us-east-1 · Versioning **Enabled** (MFA delete off) · SSE-S3 AES256 · Public Access Block: all 4 true · no lifecycle · no notifications.
- Top level: `All Loans - City Lending Inc.xlsx` (145 MB, 2026-07-06) + 43 prefixes: `Adverse Loans 2018/`…`2025/`, `Archive ATBM/`, `Archive EEP Loans/`, `Archive Trio Loans/`, `Closed Loans 2018 - Banked/` … `Closed Loans 2025 - Wholesale/` (Banked/Broker/Wholesale per year), `Data Points/`, `My Pipeline/`, `Prospect - Old/`, `Prospects/`, `TPO Adverse Loans 2021/2023/2024/2025`, `TPO Pipeline - Submitted/`, `Trio Pipeline/`, `_textract-input/`, `_textract-output/`.
- Versioning + no lifecycle on 2.4 TB means deleted/overwritten objects are retained forever (cost).

**`integrator-upload-documents`** (2020-10-27) — 30 GB / 45k objects, 14,661 top-level `folder-<uuid>/` prefixes (Encompass eFolder upload integration).
- Versioning Enabled · AES256 · **Public Access Block: all 4 FALSE** (no bucket policy, ACL = owner only, so not actually public today, but unguarded) · no lifecycle · no notifications.

**`appraisal-xml`** (2021) — 21 GB / 9,377 appraisal XMLs; per-user prefixes `admin/ cbonell/ clgutierrez/ jcuen/ lconsuegra/ mdiaz/ mrodriguez/ murilloa/ vrueda/`. Versioning Enabled, PAB all true, no notifications.

**`automatic-order-voe`** — 4 GB; `Temporary/` + loose `Title-<loan#>-*.pdf` files (VOE/title order documents). No notifications.

**`encompass-supreme-backup-prod`** — `backups/` + `failed-records/`; **S3 event → Lambda `supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod-processFile`** on `ObjectCreated` for `backups/*.xlsx` (Encompass→Salesforce sync trigger). Versioning off.

**`be-in-mortgage-broker-package`** — **bucket policy IsPublic = true** (static site: `index.html` + `Be In Mortgage - Broker package/`; also served via Amplify app `da33la3u9ax44`).

**`supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod-site-443216489626`** — SPA (`index.html app.js config.js styles.css`) behind CloudFront `E3SX47YFC9Q8RE` (`d1hilykves4b9p.cloudfront.net`); policy not public (OAC).

**`general-files-storage`** — `chatb2b/` prefix. **`signature-creator-supreme`** — email-signature images (Amplify `signatureCreator`). **`my-etl-*`** — read by EC2 DataRepository instance role.

CloudWatch has **668 `/aws/lambda/*` log groups for ~93 distinct stacks** — the majority reference functions that no longer exist (pipelinemanagementtool, encompass-webhook-manager, update-queue, queue, uw-conditions, lo-compensations, payroll, turn-on-times, jets-preheat…). The historical City Lending platform was far larger than what remains.

### 8. Security observations (for the handover, not fixed)

1. **Root access key exists** (`AccountAccessKeysPresent=1`) and this scan itself ran as root — the root key is in use on the departing admin's machine.
2. `serverless` IAM user (2019) has **AdministratorAccess with two active keys**, one used minutes ago from a DynamoDB client. This is the long-lived credential embedded in old City Lending apps/servers; no one knows every place it lives.
3. `serverless-deploy-user` key is used by CI (GitHub) — needed for deploys of the `salesforce-lambda-bridge` / `supreme-encompass-salesforce` stacks.
4. Dormant user keys: `jposso` (unused since 2024-11), `smera` (unused since 2025-04).
5. Console users with Admin: mrodriguez (departing), cadelgado, lvargas. Dev_Team_CL is near-admin (S3 full, DatabaseAdministrator, EC2 full, Amplify admin).
6. All 3 RDS instances are **publicly accessible** (security-group protected only).

8. `be-in-mortgage-broker-package` bucket policy is public (intentional static site).
9. Plaintext secrets in Lambda env: `bim-blue-payment-prod-*` (`ENCOMPASS_PASSWORD`), `salesforce-to-meta-sync-dev-createLead` (`META_ACCESS_TOKEN`).
10. `blue-payment-prod` runs **nodejs12.x** (EOL).
11. Org management account `202294324213` uses a personal Gmail as master email; `OrganizationAccountAccessRole` trusts it (no policy attached today).
12. Six scheduled EventBridge rules fire daily at deleted Lambdas (silent failures).

### 9. What breaks when the departing admin (mrodriguez) is removed

**IAM user `mrodriguez@citylendinginc.com` itself: nothing breaks.** It has no access keys; it is a console-only Admin_CL member (MFA `1P`). Removing it removes nothing that automation depends on.

**What is actually tied to that person and WILL break / needs re-homing:**
- **Root credentials + root access key** — used for this scan; if the root key lives only on their laptop/1Password, rotate/delete it and move root MFA + recovery email to a company-controlled mailbox. Org master email `grioslecca@gmail.com` also needs to move.
- **`serverless-deploy-user` access key `AKIAWOMO************`** — lives in GitHub Actions secrets in the `City-Lending` / `salesForceCityLendingInc` / `HomeSi-Latino` GitHub orgs. If those GitHub orgs/repos are owned by the departing admin's GitHub account, **deploys of salesforce-lambda-bridge, supreme-encompass-salesforce, data-quality-api, refi, meta-sync stop**. Runtime is unaffected.
- **`serverless` user key `AKIAWOMO************`** — actively hitting DynamoDB right now from an unknown client (likely the EC2 boxes, a local script, or Serverless Dashboard). If it is rotated without finding the caller, that client breaks; if the caller is the admin's workstation, it simply stops.
- **Serverless Dashboard (org `SLS_ORG_ID`, roles trusting 622911592858 / 802587217904)** — the Serverless Inc account/org is registered under a person; monitoring, `serverless_lambda_deploy_events` and log ingestion stop when that seat is closed. Runtime is unaffected.
- **Amplify GitHub connections** for the 4 repo-linked apps — auto-deploy on push breaks if the OAuth token was the admin's.
- **SES identity `m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`** — any Lambda/flow sending "from" that address fails verification once the mailbox is gone; `send-email-prod-*` functions should be checked for hardcoded From.
- **Salesforce integration user credentials** stored in Secrets Manager (`salesforce-lambda-bridge-prod`, `supreme-encompass-salesforce-prod`, `integration-encompass-salesforce-prod`, `RDS_*`) — if they contain the admin's personal Salesforce/Encompass logins (the n8n CLAUDE.md notes F-16: "Personal login — replace with integration user"), the Encompass→Salesforce sync (`processFile`) and the LeadView portal API break when those logins are deactivated. This is the highest-probability real outage.
- **`appraisal-xml/mrodriguez/`** prefix and the FHA-binder `loanrepository` workflow run from the admin's workstation with root/admin creds — the process, not the data, leaves with them.
- Remaining admins after removal: `cadelgado` (last login Jan 2026) and `lvargas` (last login Sep 2025) — both stale; confirm at least one is active before deleting the departing user.

#### What breaks if a Google account is removed

* **`itsupportaccount@simologic.com`** (the account the BigQuery connector, the `salesforce` dataset and — by creation date — the Salesforce transfer config belong to):
  * The DTS transfer config is owned by the user who created it. Deleting that Google user (or revoking its BigQuery rights) does **not** stop the service agent from running loads, but the transfer's Salesforce OAuth credential (connected-app client id/secret + Salesforce user) was authorised **by that person**; Google disables/fails transfers whose owner credentials become invalid, and nobody else can edit or re-authorise the config without re-creating it. Treat the transfer as owned by this account until the console (BigQuery → Data transfers → config → "Update credentials" / "Transfer owner") says otherwise.
  * The Claude BigQuery connector, the "HOMESÍ Analytics Agent" (Conversational Analytics, Agents Hub) and the `home_si` glossary/serving views' ownership go with it.
  * If the transfer stops: `salesforce.*` freezes → **every** view in `home_si`, `app_b2b_metrics`, `b2b_marts` and most of `lending_marts` (79 views, see §7 dependency map) silently go stale; the Lovable app (`lovable-bq-reader`) and the Metrics B2B/SimoOS app (`simo-sync-bq-reader`) keep working on stale data with no error.
* **`icano@simologic.com`**: nothing scheduled runs as this account (all writes are interactive MCP sessions), so no pipeline stops. But it is the only human OWNER of `app_b2b_metrics`, `b2b_marts`, `comp_marts`, `governance`, `hr_centralizado`, `lending_marts`; the `projectOwners` group still has OWNER, so access is recoverable via project IAM. All view SQL is captured in Appendix A of this file as insurance.
* **Service accounts** (`simo-sync-bq-writer/reader`, `lovable-bq-reader`, `b2b-metrics-sync`, `simoos-app-bigquery`) are project-native and unaffected by user removal — **but their JSON keys / WIF bindings live in the apps (Lovable, Vercel `simo-os.env` WIF handoff, SimoOS uploader)**; those are the credentials to inventory next.
* Not checkable via SQL and still to confirm in the console: DTS config owner & Salesforce credential user, Agents Hub agent owner, project IAM (who else is `roles/owner`), billing account owner.

All datasets: location `US`, `maxTimeTravelHours` 168, case-sensitive, no labels, no default table expiration, no CMEK.
`SCHEMATA_OPTIONS` confirms the same (location, descriptions, `default_partition_expiration_days = 60.0` on `governance`).

Service accounts seen (all in project `mcp-connector-procedure`):
`simo-sync-bq-writer`, `simo-sync-bq-reader`, `lovable-bq-reader`, `b2b-metrics-sync`, `simoos-app-bigquery`, plus the Google-managed `service-804513710204@gcp-sa-bigquerydatatransfer`.

### 2. Tables and views (metadata)

Row count / size from `__TABLES__` (views show 0). Created / last-modified are BigQuery metadata. No table is partitioned or clustered except
`hr_centralizado.active_roster_raw` and `hr_centralizado.employee_draws_us_raw` (ingestion-style partitioning on `uploaded_at`, `require_partition_filter=false`).

#### 4.4 DTS run calendar (last 30 days) — 10 loads per run, 0 errors

Runs at 05:02–05:06 UTC on every day 2026-08-17 → 2026-09-11. **No run on 2026-09-12 or 2026-09-13.** One run at **2026-09-14 15:13 UTC**
(run id `6addfae2-0000-2ddb-a115-3c286d3c9902`, off-schedule → manual "Run transfer now" or a retried run), then 2026-09-15 05:02 as normal.
The two skipped days are not visible in JOBS (a DTS run that fails before the load stage, e.g. Salesforce auth/API error, produces no BigQuery job) —
**check the run history in the console (BigQuery → Data transfers → config `6a6b8803…` → Run history) for the 09-12/09-13 error message; it is the most likely place a credential problem shows first.**
Bytes per load ≈ table size (Task 1.26 GB, Lead 194 MB each day) → the transfer is a **full refresh (WRITE_TRUNCATE)**, not incremental.

#### 4.6 Data Transfer Service configuration

Not readable via SQL/MCP (no `bq ls --transfer_config` equivalent in this toolset). Known from job labels:
`data_source_id = salesforce`, `dts_config_id = 6a6b8803-0000-2016-a2f4-582429a8c29c`, destination dataset `salesforce`, 10 objects, daily ~05:02 UTC, full refresh,
service agent `service-804513710204@gcp-sa-bigquerydatatransfer.iam.gserviceaccount.com` (WRITER on `salesforce`). **Owner, notification settings, Salesforce
connected-app client id and the Salesforce user behind the OAuth grant must be read in the console: BigQuery → Data transfers → `6a6b8803…` → Configuration.**
The dataset was created by / is owned by `itsupportaccount@simologic.com` on 2026-06-12 21:30 and the first load ran 2026-06-13 05:01, so the config was
almost certainly created under `itsupportaccount@simologic.com`. Salesforce side: permission set `BigQuery_Export_Read_All` (per project docs) should be assigned to the
integration user used by the transfer — confirm it is not the departing admin's personal Salesforce user.

### 1. `fha-lrs-binder` — FHA LRS post-endorsement case binder assembly

**Files**
- `SKILL.md` — 8,845 B
- `references/lrs-order-and-lessons.md` — 12,547 B (authoritative LRS stacking order, copy-selection rules, ~12 numbered lessons, Selection Verification Protocol)
- `scripts/qc_verify.py` — 8,562 B (deterministic QC engine: cross-loan page detection, junk sweep, duplicate N-page-doc collapse, allonge/CD/92900-A checks, purchase-vs-refi guess)
- `scripts/assemble_binder.py` — 4,221 B (plan JSON → binder PDF + 6-column Excel build sheet; PyMuPDF, `garbage=3, deflate=False`, builds in halves on `/tmp`, verifies `%%EOF`)
- `scripts/build_ocr_cache.py` — 2,555 B (Textract async output blocks → `page_text_<LOAN>.json`; supports multi-part loans via `--parts dir:pagecount`)

**Purpose:** Turn a 1,000–3,000-page combined scanned loan eFolder (with drafts, duplicates, cross-loan pages, emails) into a clean, ordered FHA LRS binder — exactly one selected copy of each required document in the authoritative stacking order — plus an Excel build sheet mapping every binder page to its source page. Compliance-sensitive; reviewed by humans.

**External systems / credentials**
- **AWS API MCP** (`mcp__AWS_API_MCP_Server__call_aws`, on the remote device) — S3 `list-objects-v2`, `get-object`/sync, `presign`; Textract `start-document-text-detection` / `get-document-text-detection`. Requires AWS credentials with read on `loanrepository` and write on the `_textract-output/` prefix, plus Textract permissions, in **us-east-1**.
- Bash sandbox with PyMuPDF (`fitz`), `openpyxl`, `qpdf`, Python.
- A connected (Cowork) output folder mount — noted as slow (~45 s command window, >120 MB writes truncate).

**Hardcoded identifiers**
- S3 bucket: **`loanrepository`** (region **`us-east-1`**)
- Textract output prefix: **`_textract-output/<LOAN#>`**
- Project state file name: **`textract_jobs.csv`** (lives in the project/connected folder, not the skill)
- Loan-number regex in `qc_verify.py`: `main[a-z]{2}\d{8} | jcat\d{8} | jcgr\d{10} | [a-z]{2}\d{11} | \d{8}` (example loan ids cited: `jv05324031459`, `rj06824020826`, `ef61380024594`, `jv05323034400`, `JCAT4222099662-1..-10`)
- Lender brand used for junk sweep: **"City Lending"** (cover letters, UW decisions, brand header pages are removed); credit vendor **Birchwood**.
- Example hardcoded lesson names: allonge officer "HECTOR T. GARTON / OPERATIONS MANAGER" (OCR-mangled-signature lesson).

**What breaks when the departing admin's accounts are removed:** the AWS credential behind the AWS API MCP (the remote-device MCP server on the admin's machine). Without an IAM identity that can read `loanrepository`, write `_textract-output/`, and call Textract, steps 2–3 and the presign step are dead. The scripts themselves are portable. The `textract_jobs.csv` / OCR caches live in the admin's connected local folder and will be lost with the machine unless copied. Nothing here touches Salesforce or M365.


* * *

### 2. `recruitment-copilot` — MMI + NMLS Loan Officer recruitment qualification

**Files:** `SKILL.md` only — 79,051 B (very long; contains all JS extraction snippets inline). Memory companion: `/areas/recruitment-copilot.md` (3,241 B) in the user's Claude memory store.

**Purpose:** Autonomous daily batch qualification of **Loan Officer** Leads. For each lead: read MMI production/license/agent data, apply a license check + 5 gates, look up NMLS Consumer Access employment, and write a single atomic outcome (Ready to Call / Call Later / Disqualified / Skipped) to Salesforce. Caps at **30 READY_TO_CALL** per session, **600** leads processed safety cap. Every READY_TO_CALL lead is reassigned (`OwnerId`) to the session operator.

**External systems / credentials**
- **Salesforce hosted MCP** (`getUserInfo`, `soqlQuery`, `updateSobjectRecord`, `createSobjectRecord`) — the skill notes the MCP "typically runs under a shared integration/service account, not the human running the session". In this environment that is the `Salesforce - sObject prod` connector. Salesforce DX tools are forbidden.
- **Chrome** (Claude in Chrome / `javascript_tool`) for **MMI** (`new.mmi.run`, human login) and **NMLS Consumer Access** (human passes CAPTCHA). No API for either.
- Downstream Salesforce automation: autolaunched flow **`Realtor_AI_Profile`** fires on `AI_Trigger__c` and populates `AI_NMLS_Number__c`, `AI_NMLS_Profile_URL__c`, `AI_Loan_Products__c`, `AI_employerType__c`, `AI_Status__c`, `AI_Run_Started__c`, `AI_Last_Run__c` via HTTP callouts (the callout credential is outside this skill — check that flow's named credential).

**Hardcoded identifiers**
- Loan Officer Lead RecordTypeId **`012Kb000000RpDCIA0`**
- URLs: **`https://new.mmi.run/login`**, **`https://new.mmi.run/`**, **`https://new.mmi.run/lo/<NMLS>`** (legacy), **`https://new.mmi.run/nmls/<NMLS>`** (beta), **`https://www.nmlsconsumeraccess.org`**, **`https://www.nmlsconsumeraccess.org/EntityDetails.aspx/INDIVIDUAL/<NMLS>`**, `mmi.run/l/intelligence?nmls=<num>`
- MMI payload path **`window.__NUXT__.data.lo`** (fields `profile.FOURTEENMONTHUNITS`, `profile.ACTIVELICENSES`, `profile.ACTIVEREGISTRATION`, `profile.ACTIVESTATEARRAY`, `profile.FULLNAME`, `profile.NMLS`, `nmls_history[].Current/LicenseType/State`, `agents[]`, `ls_agents[]` with `AGENT_NAME`/`PARTNERUNITS`), DOM selectors `#ls-title-trans-tab-pane`, `.ag-center-cols-container [role="row"]`, `.multiselect-search input`, `li.multiselect-option`
- Lead fields: `Productivity_Link_FKA_MMI_Link__c`, `NMLS_Number__c`, `AI_Trigger__c`, `AI_Status__c`, `Recruitment_Status__c` (values `"Ready to Call"`, `"Call Later"`, `"Disqualified"`, `"Skipped - No MMI"`), `Last_Recruitment_Check__c`, `Next_Recruitment_Check__c`, `Recruitment_Comment__c` (255), `Latino_Name_Likelihood__c`, `Possible_Dual_License__c`, `Reasons_for_discarding__c` (`"Not Interested"`), `Branch__c` (`"Recruitment"`)
- Task Status value **`"Open"`** (not "Not Started")
- Business constants: 14–60 units / 14 months, ≥15% Latino ratio, 6 months / 15 years tenure, 30-lead cap, 600 safety cap, re-check intervals 7 d / 30 d / 6 mo / 9 mo / 12 mo
- Disqualifying employer name **"Everett Financial"** (i.e. Supreme Lending's own legal entity)
- Verified example NMLS ids cited: 580525 (bank LO), 205993, 1634703, 371377

**What breaks when the departing admin's accounts are removed**
- The Salesforce hosted MCP connector is authorized under the admin's Salesforce login (`m.rodriguez@supremelending.com`, System Administrator, or whichever account the connector was OAuth'd with). Removing it kills every read/write. Re-authorize the connector under an active user with edit on Lead + Task and the recruitment fields.
- MMI login is a personal subscription/login performed by the operator in Chrome — if the admin's MMI account is the only one, MMI access dies.
- Chrome extension (Claude in Chrome) is installed on the admin's machine; another operator needs it installed and site permissions granted for `new.mmi.run` and `nmlsconsumeraccess.org`.
- Operator assignment is per-session by email, so no leads are hardwired to the admin; but leads previously written READY_TO_CALL and owned by the admin will need reassignment.

### 3. `salesforce-lead-import` — Borrower/Realtor lead batch preparation

**Files**

### 00_README_PROJECT_SETUP.md
- **Length:** ~7 KB. **Date stated:** "accurate as of 24 August 2026". **Type:** upload manifest / project-instructions guide.
- **Summary:** Explains which of the 16 staged files to upload into the project and why, in three tiers. Tier 1 (the spine): 01 Salesforce org CLAUDE (208 KB, "single most valuable file"), 02 n8n engine CLAUDE, 03 design spec, 04 start-here index, 05 prod state log. Tier 2 (status): 06 open defects, 07 test evidence, 08 remaining cutover, 09 borrower email inventory, 09b agent guide docx (optional, 1.4 MB screenshots). Tier 3 (other org work, ~57 KB): 10 outage RCA, 11 SiMo BPO, 12 email engagement, 13 Latino name runbook, 14 BigQuery agent prompt, 15 LOA playbook docx. Lists what NOT to upload: nine superseded agent-guide versions (v1–v10; only v11 current, and older ones cite the retired `Requested_Document__c`), duplicate design spec copy, recruitment-copilot SKILL.md files (install as skills), Make.com blueprints (250–500 KB JSON each), `Realtor Worker - LO Field Update Plan.md`, all CSV/XLSX (contain borrower/realtor PII), board deck pptx, deploy scratch folders (`furprod/`, `_diff/`, `guide_screenshots_v11/`).
- **Suggested project instructions (verbatim rules worth keeping):** Salesforce is source of truth, n8n holds no durable state; read 04 first; four damage-causing rules — (1) a "fixed" claim is worthless unless raw metadata re-read after write in active version, (2) a pinned n8n test never validates a query/credential/send, (3) a deploy's `created:true|false` is the only authoritative existence check for a SF field, (4) editing an active n8n workflow creates a draft — not live until publish and version IDs match. "This system's characteristic failure mode is silence, not errors." `fur_settings.test_mode = true` is a live kill switch — never change without explicit instruction.
- **Stale/superseded flags:** says the fastest-changing files are 05, 06, 08 — re-upload after any work. Total 2 MB / 16 files (project actually holds 25 incl. CIO/recruitment/lead-import docs added later that day).

### 03_DESIGN_SPEC.md
- **Length:** ~55 KB. **Date:** 2026-07-09, §0 status "as of 2026-07-14: DESIGN COMPLETE, NOTHING BUILT". **Type:** authoritative design spec (Borrower Follow-Up System).
- **Summary:** Specifies the Borrower Follow-Up System: an n8n engine that talks to borrowers (email-first, SMS Phase 2) and pulls agents in only when needed; Salesforce holds all state in `Follow_Up_Request__c` (FUR). Sized against prod 2026-07-09: 23,919 New + 4,061 Working unconverted Borrower leads, 23,167 (83%) with no activity ever, only 15 with `SMS_Opt_In__c = true`. Defines the data model (FUR object ~24 fields; `Requested_Document__c` for Lead-stage docs; 8–10 new fields on `Mortgage_Condition__c` for Opp-stage chase incl. `Chase_With_Borrower__c`, `Borrower_Doc_Ask__c` Text(255), `Chase_Latest_Note__c`, `Chase_State__c`; Lead/Opp fields `AI_Followup_Request__c`, `AI_Followup_Status__c`, `SMS_Consent_Source__c`, `SMS_Consent_At__c`), the state machine (Active → Awaiting Reply → Docs Partial/Completed/Escalated/Dead/Opted Out/Cancelled), four cadences (Docs Standard d0/+2/+5/+9; Interest d0/+3/+7; Appointment d0/+2/+5; Reactivation d0/+4/+10), six n8n workflows WF-1..WF-6, AI components (Claude via Anthropic), compliance gates, visibility/reporting design, phased rollout, 10 open decisions and a 20-finding QA register (F-1..F-20).
- **DECIDED markers:** 2026-07-10 reuse existing conditions list + checkbox, no new RT; 2026-07-10 LOA1 joins work items paired with SLA v2; 2026-07-10 Claude/Anthropic for AI; 2026-07-10 SMS two-tier consent (Opp stage = application is consent, Lead stage asks); 2026-07-10 `Chase_Latest_Note__c` separate from LOA's `Latest_Note__c`, one shared `Notes__c` history with 🤖 prefix; quiet hours 09:00–19:00 borrower-local Mon–Sat, Sun off, 9–11am jitter.
- **QA register key items to build as written:** F-1 poll `Awaiting Reply`; F-4 auto-reply loop guard (≤3/thread/24h, `Auto-Submitted`); F-5 mark-then-send; F-6 reactivation from separate mailbox; F-7 ambiguous match = human; F-8 Opp has no `HasOptedOutOfEmail` (later solved by custom `HasOptedOutOfEmail__c`); F-9 prompt-injection containment; F-12 no watermark in n8n (`Chase_Processed__c` checkbox); F-13 escalation resume = now+1bd same step (later overridden by user 2026-08-11 to ADVANCE one step); F-16 personal SF login must be replaced by integration user; F-18 FUR OWD → Public Read Only (prod actually deployed Private).
- **Open decisions (§12, as of spec):** reactivation cutoff/cap, mailbox name, 360 SMS API guide, WF-6 default branch, pilot branch/agents, `iAgent` licence, LOA1→LOA2 condition ownership, AI data posture for borrower docs (zero-retention terms).
- **Stale:** §0 "nothing built" is superseded by 05/08; `Requested_Document__c` was retired 2026-08-11 (docs are `Mortgage_Condition__c` work items on Lead too); mailbox `docs@simosolutionsgroup.com` superseded by `docs@homesi.co` (DECIDED 2026-08-11); credential `dsfRbykdxZPdoIte` superseded by `quBZzjgLYxZSi9My`.

### 06_OPEN_DEFECTS.md (a.k.a. `FUR_Bucket_2_3_Checklist.md`)
- **Length:** ~30 KB. **Dates:** created 2026-08-12, updated 08-17/18/19. **Type:** defect register (Buckets 2 & 3; Bucket 1 complete).
- **Summary:** Honest register of what is still wrong or unbuilt in the FUR system. Header rule: trust this over `CLAUDE.md`'s OPEN list — seven "OPEN" items there were already built. 2026-08-17 campaign: WF-4 architecture changed — `FUR WF-4 Inbound Dispatcher` (`pA2IVtxuaTyFIQGK`) now owns the Outlook poll, screens auto-replies, invokes WF-4 with `mode: each`; WF-4's trigger is an `executeWorkflowTrigger` still named `New Email (docs inbox)`. Test harness `FUR TEST - Inbound Email Simulator` (`66oCRAyOwmBfwLnk`) — never activate in prod; cannot attach files. Fixed 08-17: WF-4 dropped all but first reply per poll (exec 328 → fixed 331–337); blank subject token matched a NULL-token FUR (`a1KEm00001TOh5pMAD`, now Cancelled); mailbox pollution (Claude marketing, OTP, Microsoft notices all escalated — likely drained Anthropic credits) → F-4 auto-reply/OOO guard built in dispatcher (`intent = auto_reply_skipped`); attachments on unroutable replies rescued to Lead/Opp Files (`fur_question_log` gained `has_attachments`, `message_id`). Fixed 08-18: WF-5 exhaust `Close_NoResponse` failed `/dead/i` → exact vocabulary `Escalate` / `Dead_Lead_OnHold` / `Close_NoResponse`, throws on unknown, live-loan safety floor (never Dead with open Opp) — exec 382.
- **DECIDED:** 08-17 do NOT escalate every exhaust; closing = FUR only, never Lead status; 08-18 keep unknown exhaust action STRICT (throw) and observe.
- **Config-that-lies (traced 08-19):** `Build Messages` reads only 5 of 12 `fur_settings` keys (`test_mode`, `test_email`, `fallback_tz`, `quiet_start_hour`, `quiet_end_hour`). Silently ignored but populated: `freq_cap_email_24h`=1, `freq_cap_sms_24h`=1, `dedupe_hours`=4, `jitter_start_hour`=9, `jitter_end_hour`=11, `send_days`=Mon-Sat, `reactivation_daily_cap`=100.
- **OPEN Bucket 2 (borrower-visible):** frequency cap ≤1 email/person/24h not built; 4h dedupe not built; `send_days` not read (Sun hardcoded); quiet hours single-TZ (ET); 9–11am jitter not built; WF-2 `outcome` lies on blank-ask branch (latent); Lead work items null `Agent_Role__c`; Message-ID match rung not built; WF-4 `Get FUR` lacks `alwaysOutputData` (lowest priority; correct fix = alwaysOutputData + If guard → Log Unmatched); Thread_Token required/VR; sender allowlist; header-based auto-reply check; backfill `backfill_workitem_lta.apex` for 14 staging work items with null LTA (run in prod after cutover); 4 duplicate `AI Follow-Up - FUR-OPPTEST1` items in staging; wording-item owner fallback `005Em00000E99YfIAJ` only path exercised.
- **Cadence observations:** Reactivation 14d/3 touches runs longer than Docs Standard 12d/4 touches; five rows specify `SMS_or_Email`/`Email_SMS` but SMS not built — enabling SMS will silently change existing cadences.
- **Closed/downgraded 08-19:** `Owning_Role__c` write-only (no readers); blank-ask untick is NOT a defect (do not "fix"); partial blank-ask fixed (`Needs borrower wording` work item, exec 387); blank task name in widget fixed (`Condition_Name_LTA__c` now written); multi-intent classification fixed (exec 388, booleans `opt_out`/`not_interested`/`has_question`/`wants_call`/`will_send_docs`; `intent` keeps single-label for `Opted Out?` branch); `Log Unmatched` empty strings fixed; two keyless `customFieldsValues` proven working (so the CLAUDE.md "keyless = broken" rule is over-broad).
- **Bucket 3 (untested):** no unattended multi-touch cadence run (WF-1 A/B, WF-2, WF-5 inactive; dispatcher+WF-4 have run unattended 16–17 Aug); proposed 1-week soak with `test_mode=true`; volume unknown (`LIMIT 200`); FURs never close on Opp close; escalation rot-guard >2bd not built; Task logging in WF-2 not built; SMS entirely unbuilt; AI personalization not built; credential bindings not auditable via MCP, two creds named "Salesforce - Prod".
- **MCP server ids:** `4bed0244…` = STAGING (`m.rodriguez@supremelending.com.staging`); `0f15eecd…` = PRODUCTION.
- **Cross-cutting rules:** never `setNodeParameter`; re-read raw node JSON in activeVersion after any write; never clear SF field with literal `""` (bind expression); `create_workflow_from_code`/`addNode` auto-bind "Salesforce - Prod" — rebind to Sandbox `8j2Z1Q8YwHHvR9nn` (stale now that prod is the target); find-or-create tests need ≥2 targets, shuffled rows, one zero-row target.

WF-4 + Dispatcher (`7nVnALsJHaPseTVt`, `pA2IVtxuaTyFIQGK`) execs 328, 331–337, 352, 357, 360, 364, 370, 373, 376, 378, 381, 388 — all live via real email.
- **13 bugs found:** multi-email drop; blank token matched NULL-token FUR; mailbox pollution; attachments lost on unroutable replies; dedupe blind to untyped rows (`guessType`); blank-ask "All Docs Received"; sub collapsed N FURs to 1; `Set Escalated` never cleared Next_Touch; Lead work-item dedupe asymmetric; F-4 guard never built; signature blocks in agent text; Reactivation `/dead/i` + no unknown branch; self-inflicted "Hi there" regex regression. ### 09_BORROWER_EMAIL_INVENTORY.md
- **Length:** ~12 KB. **Date:** 20 Aug 2026. **Type:** marketing/compliance brief with verbatim template inventory (from WF-2 `nG8hwck9RsoJZiQb`).
- **Summary:** 20 email bodies + 20 subjects at launch (Docs Standard 4 steps, Interest 3, Appointment 3 × EN/ES); Reactivation 6 more in Phase 2 from a separate mailbox. Hard constraints: `[FUR-xxx]` token at end of subject is untouchable; doc list is AI-generated per borrower; only merge field is first name; HTML only, no plain-text part; sent from the connected mailbox. Current email = intro paragraph + "Here is what we still need:" / "Esto es lo que aun necesitamos:" + bullets + footer; no header/logo/button/address.
- **Compliance problems (must fix before any borrower email):** 4.1 sends from `itsupportaccount@simosolutionsgroup.com` while signed "Homesi" (target `docs@homesi.co` not live at writing); 4.2 footer renders `Homesi · NMLS #—.` literally — licensed lender is Everett Financial, Inc. dba Supreme Lending; 4.3 footer promises unsubscribe link that does not exist (biggest exposure); 4.4 "Reply STOP to opt out of texts" on email-only sends; 4.5 "Homesi" not "Homesí", no "powered by Supreme Lending"; 4.6 Spanish templates unaccented (`prestamo`, `aun`, no `¿`) while AI doc lines are accented; 4.7 no-name fallback reads "Hola there,".
- **Verbatim copy** for all four cadences is in the doc (e.g. Docs d0 EN "Documents needed for your loan" / "Hi [NAME], to keep your loan moving we still need the documents below…"; d+2 mentions "text us a photo" — SMS not built; d+9 "avoid a hold on your file" needs compliance sign-off; Interest uses SMS-style "Reply 1/2/3").
- **Send timing:** 09:00–19:00 Eastern for every borrower (not borrower-local), Mon–Sat; reply resets +2bd; question stops the sequence.

### 10_PROD_OUTAGE_RCA.md
- **Length:** ~5 KB. **Date:** 2026-07-28 (incident). **Type:** Salesforce Support case write-up (Sev 1).

Org `00DKb000000OvoRMAS`, `ruby-ruby-7485.my.salesforce.com`. Example record `006Qg00000kz2lcIAA`. ErrorIds: recurring core `530106887`; `1924604064-522748`, `1486507758-96614`, `1751704383-80896`, `1087300778-350767`, `621161304-296332`; downstream flow faults `327806419-78910`, `501570063-300660`. ApexLogs `07LQg00000XIbxPMAT`, `07LQg00000XIbxOMAT` show Status=Success — failure at commit stage. Permission-isolation tests ruled out Contact/Opp object access; Leads saved fine. Timeline: Contact OWD → Private (metadata), sharing rule `Contact_All_Internal_Read` created, reverted ~4–5 PM, recalcs completed 5:13/5:21 PM, error persisted. **The case's working hypothesis (inconsistent sharing state) was WRONG** — per 01_SALESFORCE_ORG_CLAUDE gotcha #30 the real cause was 8 LOA2 UW-stage flows using cross-object `RecordType.DeveloperName` in entry criteria (null field reference); fixed 2026-07-29 with `RecordTypeId = 012Kb000000RpDEIA0`. This doc is the *as-filed* case, not the resolution. Contact: Melquiades Rodriguez, m.rodriguez@supremelending.com.
