### 12_EMAIL_ENGAGEMENT_DESIGN.md
- **Length:** ~9 KB. **Date:** 2026-07-02, "Design only (nothing built)". **Type:** design.
- **Summary:** Email engagement score from Customer.io events, which land in Salesforce as **Task** records with `Task.Subtype__c` (all-time prod counts: Email Sent 1,350,565; Email Opened 243,364; Email Link Clicked 75,540; Email Failed 18,851; Email Unsubscribed 5,009; Marked as Spam 60; SMS 61,884). Blocker: no campaign id on Tasks (no `campaign_id`/`broadcast_id`/`message_id` field; Subject is personalised message subject; `Email Sent` mixes CIO and rep emails via Einstein Activity Capture). Salesforce Campaigns are Facebook lead-gen campaigns, not CIO campaigns — no shared key, so per-campaign scoring is blocked on an integration change. Proposed weights Sent 1 / Opened 3 / Clicked 5 / Spam −10; tiers Hot ≥30 & recent, Warm 10–29, Cold <10, Dormant. Level 1 person score buildable now: fields `Email_Sent_Count__c`, `Email_Opened_Count__c`, `Email_Clicked_Count__c`, `Email_Engagement_Score__c`, `Last_Email_Engagement__c`, `Email_Engagement_Tier__c` on Lead+Contact, maintained by Task after-insert flow + Apex batch backfill. Level 2 needs `CIO_Campaign_Id__c` on Task or (preferred) object `CIO_Email_Event__c`, plus `Campaign.CIO_Campaign_Id__c` ExtId and junction `Campaign_Engagement__c`; overall = AVG(campaign scores). Recommends scoring Opened+Clicked only. **No Customer.io workspace/sync ids in this doc**; Salesforce field mapping is the Task `Subtype__c` values above. Open decisions: score basis, weights, Leads vs Contacts, recency decay, integration owner.

Whether this was ever deployed to prod is not recorded anywhere in the project.

### 14_BIGQUERY_AGENT_PROMPT.md  ← FULL extraction of BigQuery facts
- **Length:** ~10 KB. **Date:** undated. **Type:** system-instructions prompt for a BigQuery Conversational Analytics agent ("HOMESÍ Analytics Agent").
- **BigQuery project:** `mcp-connector-procedure`. **Dataset:** `salesforce`. Daily-synced replicated copy of Salesforce (scheduled transfer, up to ~24h behind). Flat tables, no relationship traversal.
- **Tables loaded (6):** `Opportunity` (loan/deal), `Lead`, `Contact`, `User`, `Profile`, `Loan_Officer__c`. **NOT loaded:** `RecordType`, `Account` — filter by literal `RecordTypeId`.
- **Join keys:** `Opportunity.OwnerId`→`User.Id`; `Opportunity.Loan_Officers__c`→`Loan_Officer__c.Id`; `Loan_Officer__c.Salesforce_User__c`→`User.Id`; `User.ProfileId`→`Profile.Id`; `Lead.OwnerId`→`User.Id`.
- **Schema caveats:** soft-deleted rows present — always `IsDeleted = FALSE` on Opportunity/Lead/Contact; percent fields 100/0/NULL so `AVG(On_Time_Pct__c)` = %; `CloseDate` and `Disbursement_Date__c` kept in sync (prefer Disbursement).
- **Canonical filters:** Borrower Opp RT `012Kb000000RpDEIA0`; closed loans = `IsDeleted=FALSE AND RecordTypeId='012Kb000000RpDEIA0' AND Closed_Loan__c=TRUE AND Lender__c LIKE '%Eve%' AND Current_Status__c != 'Archive Loan'`; active pipeline = `Closed_Loan__c=FALSE AND Application_Date__c IS NOT NULL AND Org_Est_Closing_Date__c IS NOT NULL`; Lead RTs Borrower `012Kb000000RpDAIA0`, Realtor `012Kb000000RpDDIA0`, Broker `012Kb000000RpDBIA0`, Loan Officer `012Kb000000RpDCIA0`; active leads = `IsConverted=FALSE AND Status NOT IN ('Discarded')`.
- **Key fields:** `Branch__c`, `Current_Milestone__c` (Started→Processing→Submittal→Initial Decision→Resubmittal→Clear To Close→Closing), `Loan_Channel__c`, `Loan_Type__c`, `Amount`, `Pre_Approved_Date__c`, `Ratified_Date__c`, `Stat_Closing_Risk__c`, `Healthiness__c` (source of truth over Stat risk), `Closing_On_Time__c`, `On_Time_Pct__c`, `Org_Est_Closing_Date__c`.
- **Business context:** HOMESÍ = Supreme Lending's Latino growth division; lender of record Everett Financial, Inc. dba Supreme Lending; goal 30% Latino market share by 2028. Tiers Core/Growth/Elite (cumulative, quarterly): On-Time Closings ≥95/97/99%; Pull-Through ≥80/85/90%; SLA ≥95/97/99%; CRM accuracy 100% all tiers; Production from Database ≥20/25/35%. Prospecting KPIs: realtor calls/day 8/10/12; meetings/week 5/7/10; App→Approval 30/35/40%; Referral conversion 60/65/70%; NPS client 75/80/85, realtor 80/85/90, partner 75/80/85. Single targets: email open ≥30%, click ≥5%, DB lead conversion ≥10%, automation adoption ≥95%, activity logging ≥95%, partner retention ≥90%. Cadence: KPIs weekly, LO scorecards monthly, tier reviews quarterly. Kickoff deck with quotas is NOT available to the agent.
- **No agent id / deployment status recorded** — doc is the prompt text to paste into BigQuery Agents Hub.

### CIO-Salesforce_Subscription_Redesign_DRAFT.md
- **Length:** ~9 KB. **Date:** 2026-05-21, status EXECUTED. **Type:** design + as-built for Customer.io → Salesforce destination `52375`, workspace `164380`.
- **Summary:** Problem: both syncs write SF `Id` into the same CIO `user_id`, so profiles are Lead ids (`00Q…`) or Opp ids (`006…`); the six Task subscriptions blindly put `userId` into `WhoId`, which rejects Opp ids (`FIELD_INTEGRITY_EXCEPTION`, continuous `ERRORS_DISCARDED_400`). Fix: split each of 6 email events into a Lead branch (`!match( properties.userId, "006*" )`, `WhoId`) and an Opportunity branch (`match(...)`, `WhatId`), plus opt-out updates. FQL `match` is glob full-string. Hardcoded Task `OwnerId` `005Kb00000B1ZEtIAN`, `TaskSubtype Email`, `ActivityDate` from `$.timestamp`.
- **As-built ids (14 subscriptions):** modified Lead branch `182232` Email Sent, `342652` Opened, `343605` Unsubscribed, `343606` Link Clicked, `343607` Marked as Spam, `343608` Failed; new Opp branch `712640` Sent, `712641` Opened, `712642` Link Clicked, `712643` Spam, `712644` Failed, `712645` Unsubscribed; `712646` Lead Email Opt Out (`HasOptedOutOfEmail: true`, matcher `traits.Id`); `712647` Opportunity Email Opt Out (`HasOptedOutOfEmail__c: true`).
- **Field mappings:** Lead `HasOptedOutOfEmail` (standard), Opportunity `HasOptedOutOfEmail__c` (custom). Per-event Subject templates (e.g. `Email Opened - {{properties.subject}}`, Failed uses `properties.failure_message`).
- **Still to do by user:** delete stale destinations `13908` (disabled, 2 stale subs) and `13990` (enabled, 0 subs); verify boolean `true` vs string `"true"` on the opt-out checkbox with live traffic.

### CLAUDE Recruitment.md
- **Length:** ~9 KB. **Date:** August 2026 (uploaded 08-25). **Type:** institutional-knowledge doc behind the `recruitment-copilot` skill.
- **Summary:** Daily batch qualification of Loan Officer Leads (org `ruby-ruby-7485`) against MMI production/license data (`window.__NUXT__.data.lo` payload-first) and NMLS employment history, via Salesforce Hosted MCP + Chrome; cap 30 READY_TO_CALL per session, assigned to the operator. Pipeline: canonical query `Status IN ('New','Working') AND Next_Recruitment_Check__c <= TODAY (or null)` → MMI → Step 2F licence check (bank/depository LO DQ, 12-month re-check) → Gate 1 units 14–60/14mo (dual-licence bypass) → Gate 2 ≥1 buyside + ≥1 listside → Gate 3 Latino agent ratio ≥15% → NMLS Gate 4 (title exclusion), Gate 5 (employer exclusion), tenure ≥6mo → READY_TO_CALL else CALL_LATER → write outcome + `Recruitment_Comment__c`. MMI payload fields: `FOURTEENMONTHUNITS`, `ACTIVELICENSES`, `ACTIVEREGISTRATION`, `ACTIVESTATEARRAY`, `nmls_history[]`, `agents[]`/`ls_agents[]`, tenure fields. Three-tier dual-licence detection (nmls_history RE row; MMI agent search; self in partner lists). 12 gotchas (never Discard a re-checkable lead — kills eligibility; payload numerics are strings/null; NMLS ≠ RE licensing; AG Grid virtualises; corroborate payload with grid before no-licence DQ; whitespace-normalise; `api.mmi.run` needs bearer token; verify typed input; strict name matching).
- **SF fields written:** `Recruitment_Status__c`, `Last_Recruitment_Check__c`, `Next_Recruitment_Check__c`, `Recruitment_Comment__c` (255), `Possible_Dual_License__c`, `OwnerId`, `Status` (only permanent DQ sets Discarded).
- **Open:** Tier-2 name collision untested; first prod session should watch lead-1 sanity check, first bank-LO DQ, first Tier-2 search; DOM-fallback suspends the bank-LO filter.

- **Summary:** Produces NEW.csv (Data Import Wizard "Add new records") and IN_QUESTION.csv (open Lead/Opp matches with Disposition column). Always ask Owner, Branch, Lead Source, Record Type, Referred By + Referred Date. Canonical ids: Lead RTs Borrower `012Kb000000RpDAIA0`, Realtor `012Kb000000RpDDIA0`, Broker `012Kb000000RpDBIA0`, Loan Officer `012Kb000000RpDCIA0`. Users: Giovanni Osorio `005Kb00000B1ZE0IAN`, Mariano Claudio `005Kb00000B1ZGaIAN`, Juseth Castro `005Qg00000Qd5lgIAB`, Javier Peñaloza `005Kb00000B1ZDCIA3`, Adriana Gonzalez `005Qg00000TxaOXIAZ`, Ana Zegarra (= "Ana Pena", ana.pena@supremelending.com) `005Qg00000Qd5lhIAB`, Adriana Romero `005Qg00000Jasa1IAB` (Co-Owner), Carolina Zazzali `005Kb00000B1ZD2IAN` (Sales Agent). `Loan_Officer__c` records: Adriana Gonzalez `a08Qg00000u7kU7IAI` (no linked user → no auto-share), Ana Zegarra `a08Qg00000FdopBIAR`. Referrer contacts: Amitay Huerta `003Kb00001XmSwMIAV`; Daniella Ottone `003Qg00000tY9lxIAC`; Lucio Romero master `003Qg00000gAkvuIAC` (266 lead + 18 opp referrals) / dup `003Qg00000qSXntIAG` (Lion Drive, 89 activities — do not delete); Manuel Solano master `003Kb00001XmSsuIAF` / dup `003Qg00000aeWJ1IAM`.
- **Field map (wizard label headers):** First Name, Last Name (required; single word → `Unknown`), Company (required, default First+Last), Email, Phone `+1 (XXX) XXX-XXXX`, **State = full name, restricted, header `State` not `State/Province`** (contradicts CLAUDE-Salesforce.md which says header "State/Province"), Lead Source, Branch (required every row), Owner ID, Record Type ID, Referred By (Contact Id, header "Referred By" not "Referred By ID"), Referred Date, Loan Officer (custom-object Id), Sales Agent (lookup filter profile IN Agent Loa On-Demand/Agent), Co-Owner, Property Address, Property Postal Code, Do Not Call, Notes → `Notes__c` (NOT `Jungo_Notes__c`), CoBorrower fields. Omit Status and Lead Importance.
- **Dedupe:** Lead phone exact formatted; Opp `Phone_360SMS__c IN` bare + `1`-prefixed; Opp email `Email__c`. Blocker = open Lead (`IsConverted=false AND Status != 'Discarded'`) or open Opp. Existing LeadSource values listed (e.g. `Realtors Base Digital`, `Juseth Database`, `Own Database`).
- **Gotchas 1–15** incl. messy source shapes, first row swallowed as header, mojibake, co-borrower pairs, double-upload creates duplicates (8/19 + 8/25 incident), FLS-hidden fields, discard rows with no email and no phone.

### 02_Data_Dictionary.md
- **Length:** ~10 KB. **Date:** generated 2026-08-25. **Type:** curated data dictionary organised by "who writes this field".
- **Summary:** Core objects Opportunity (loan), Lead, Contact, `Mortgage_Condition__c` (Work Item), `NPPM__c`, `Loan_Officer__c` (~1,600). Record type ids (identical prod/staging): Lead Borrower `012Kb000000RpDAIA0`, Broker `012Kb000000RpDBIA0`, Loan Officer `012Kb000000RpDCIA0`, Realtor `012Kb000000RpDDIA0`, SiMo BPO `012Qg000003xvgTIAQ`; Opp Borrower `012Kb000000RpDEIA0`, Realtor `012Kb000000RpDHIA0`, SiMo BPO `012Qg000003xvgUIAQ`; Work Item Mortgage Condition `012Qg000003tTm2IAE`, LOA2 Milestone Task `012Qg000003tTm1IAE`, Task `012Qg000003uX4PIAU`. Staging has an extra `Refinance` RT. All 21 Opportunity milestone date fields are Encompass-written and drive notifications #1–#12; only written on loans with `Loan__c` + `Lender__c LIKE '%Eve%'` (~2,010 open); Encompass lands several dates per save, out of order. `COC_LE_Trigger__c`/`COC_CD_Trigger__c` are Number(18,0) counters; CD has zero records populated as of 2026-08-25. People/routing: `Loan_Officers__c` 86% populated; `Loan_Processor__c` name 94% vs `Loan_Processor_Email__c` 20% (integration gap); `LOA1__c/LOA2__c` lookup filter = profile Agent Loa On-Demand. Status: Closed Won automation-only; `Current_Status__c` cleared on close; `Loan_Folder__c = "Adverse Loans"` terminal; `Branch__c` 24 active values. Deprecated: `Loan_Officer__c` singular, `Closed_Won_Date__c`, `Needs_Agent__c`/`Needs_Agent_Date__c` repurposed 2026-08-24. Lead prequal fields (Screen Flow `Prequalification_Form`); `Preferred_Language__c` marked **staging only as of 2026-08-11** (05 shows it on the prod Lead page 24 Aug — likely stale). Work Item 32 fields; OWD Private; `MortgageChecklistController` without sharing on purpose. `Notification_Settings__c` hierarchy setting `Test_Mode__c`/`Test_Recipient__c` misdirects rather than suppresses. Appendix: `FieldDefinition` reflects running user's FLS.

### Contradictions and stale claims across the project docs

1. **Prod status:** 08_REMAINING_CUTOVER header "Nothing is in prod yet" and 03_DESIGN_SPEC §0 "NOTHING BUILT" vs 05_PROD_STATE_LOG (SF metadata live 21 Aug, all 4 flows active, n8n on prod cred 24 Aug). 05 wins.
2. **05 is internally inconsistent:** §5 "Demo posture" (no n8n active, WF-4 still on staging cred) is a 21-Aug snapshot contradicted by §4c (24 Aug: WF-4 + Escalate sub active on prod; Dispatcher deliberately off). §4c wins.
3. **Duplicate "System Administrator" profile:** 05 §3.2 says `00eQg00000M4WMTIA3` no longer exists (only `00eKb000000aw4EIAQ`); 08 §1.10 and CLAUDE-Salesforce.md still say two exist.
4. **Mailbox `docs@homesi.co`:** 08 §0.3d and 09 §4.1 say not live / 404. The local n8n CLAUDE.md (not in the project) says provisioned+verified 2026-08-26/27, DKIM fixed, Dispatcher + WF-4 attachment nodes repointed to cred `X9T7HveknhfWyJd5`. Project is behind by ~3 days on this.
5. **Inbound idempotency (`Inbound_Message__c`, prod `01IQg000004QTyXMAW`, built 2026-08-27) and "Outlook trigger does NOT mark mail read"** — present only in the local CLAUDE.md files, absent from all project docs; 06/07 still describe the poll watermark as what prevents reprocessing.
6. **Multi-intent replies:** 07 "Deliberate gaps" table and 08 §4 list it as a known limitation, while 06 and 07's WF-4 table say FIXED 2026-08-19 (exec 388). Fixed wins.
7. **`Preferred_Language__c`:** 02_Data_Dictionary says "staging only as of 2026-08-11"; 05 §4b shows it on the prod Lead page (24 Aug) and lists it missing from the Opp layout — implies deployed to prod.
8. **Escalation resume step:** 03 F-13 says resume at SAME step; user decision 2026-08-11 (in 01/CLAUDE-Salesforce and 08 §1.5) ADVANCES one step.
9. **SiMo BPO build:** 11 designs reply-gated capture (nothing built); CLAUDE-Salesforce.md records the actual 2026-07-09 bulk clone of 35,829 leads.
10. **Lead import State header:** Lead_Import_CLAUDE.md says header `State` (restricted, full name); CLAUDE-Salesforce.md lead-import section says `State`→"State/Province". Lead_Import is newer and more specific.
11. **n8n credential rebind target:** 06/07 rules say "always rebind to Sandbox `8j2Z1Q8YwHHvR9nn`" — stale now that all workflows point at prod `quBZzjgLYxZSi9My`.
12. **10_PROD_OUTAGE_RCA** hypothesis (sharing inconsistency) is the as-filed guess; actual root cause (flow entry-criteria cross-object RT reference) is only in 01/CLAUDE-Salesforce gotcha #30.
13. **CIO ECA migration deadline 2026-08-17** (CIO_SimoSolutions spec §8) — never marked done; possibly related to the unresolved Lead-sync outage in CLAUDE.md (Lead sync dead since ~06-30, so predates the deadline, but worth ruling out).

**No folder is a git repository.** There is no commit history, no remote, and therefore no notion of "uncommitted work" — everything on disk is the only copy. `.sf/orgs/` source-tracking directories exist (Salesforce Workspace, CIO Workspace/apex_patch, Claude Make and N8N/sfdx_report_pull) but those are SF CLI internal tracking, not version control.

### 7. Secrets & sensitive-data hygiene

Regex scan (`AKIA…`, `sk-ant-`, `api_key=`, `password=`, `Bearer …`, `client_secret`, plus `AIza`, `ghp_`, `xox`, PRIVATE KEY, `aws_secret_access_key`, `SFDX_AUTH_URL`, `refresh_token`) across all six folders, excluding `node_modules`/`.git`:

| Hit | Path | Assessment |
|---|---|---|
| `AKIA…` access-key **ID** (root) | `Claude AWS/AWS-MCP-Setup-FHA-Binder-Submitter.md` line 155 | Key ID only (no secret present in the file); it identifies the **active root access key** the document itself says should be deleted. The file also contains the AWS account number and the org root email. Not a credential leak, but the underlying root-key situation is a real finding. |
| `aws_secret_access_key` (literal placeholder `...`) | same file, line 76 | Documentation template only. |

- **Sensitive-named files:** `BigQ/simo-os.env` (six GCP identifiers, no keys — keyless WIF). **No** `.pem`, `.key`, `credentials.json`, `service-account*.json`, `.p12` anywhere.
- `Salesforce Workspace/claude_desktop_config.json` — MCP server config (`Salesforce DX`) and Cowork preferences; no tokens.
- SF CLI auth is not in any folder (`.sf/orgs/*` contain only `localSourceTracking`).
- **PII exposure (more important than secrets here):** `FHA Binders/` holds ~2.2 GB of complete FHA loan files, signed URLAs and `Borrower Personal Info.xlsx` (SSNs, income, addresses); `Salesforce Workspace/` root holds ~50 lead-list CSV/XLSX files with real borrower/realtor names, phones, emails (Ottone, Boggio, Silva, Gonzalez, Solano, Huerta, MonitorBase alerts, etc.). Both folders are unencrypted local copies; the handover should decide retention.
- Two Apex debug logs (`apex-07LQg00000XJP9*.log`, 150 KB each) may contain record data from the July outage reproduction.


* * *

### 8. Git state

None of the six folders (nor any subfolder) contains a `.git` directory. `git remote -v` / `git status` / `git log` are therefore not applicable. Consequences for handover:
- There is no history, no diff against prod, and no backup other than the disk copies (and whatever OneDrive/Drive sync the parent `Downloads`/`Claude` folder has).
- The Salesforce org itself is the source of truth; the local tree is known to drift (see the "ALWAYS retrieve Apex from PROD before editing" rule in `CLAUDE.md`).
- Recommendation: `git init` the Salesforce Workspace `force-app`/`scripts`/`manifest`/`docs` (with a `.gitignore` for the loose CSV/XLSX lead data and logs) before anyone else edits it.
